## 5. Features, in milestone order

Every feature section has the same blocks, except that the "Tests-first plan" block is written out for M1 (5.1-5.6) and M2 (5.7-5.9); for M3 and M4 it is written when the milestone starts, from the "Windows tests that pin it" paragraph. "Register" ids (NC-1 ...) are rows of section 8; "N-Mx-yy" ids are rows of section 7. Estimated sizes: S small, M medium, L large.

**MILESTONE M1 - authoring basics**

### 5.1 New Condition (M, risk low-medium)

**What Windows does.** The Edit Condition dialog is reused on a synthetic condition (empty name, Linear, layer "Default"); the changed fields overlay the per-style table `TYPE_DEFAULTS` into a `CreateConditionSpec`, and ONE writer, `insert_condition`, runs in one transaction: bid exists; layer and folder must belong to the bid; UID = global `MAX+1` raised above every referencing column; `RefNo` = `MAX(RefNo)` of the bid + 1 (refused before any write past 2147483647 with the `Cannot add 1 condition ...` text); v4 GUID; the Windows column set is inserted. No undo entry, the new row is selected and revealed, no placement starts (`condition_action_handler.py:764-781`).

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/handlers/condition_action_handler.py:434-781` - `on_create_requested`: guards, default layer `:547-551`, synthetic condition `:552-587`, `build_spec` `:593-619`, save `:621-653`, dialog `:721-759`, after `:764-781`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/edit_condition_dialog.py:97-150` (`TYPE_DEFAULTS`), `:1255-1325` (style defaults), `:1428-1742` (validation, exact alert texts), `:1760-1839` (apply / OK / Cancel)
- `ost_visualizer/application/dtos/create_condition_spec_dto.py:5-43` - `CreateConditionSpec` and every default (no `ref_no` field)
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/condition_operations.py:254-350` - `insert_condition`; allocator and guard `:39-66` (guard `:56-62`); field map `:566-607`
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/identity_allocation.py:5-47`; `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/database/bid_owned_identity.py:105-202` (ownership checks and messages)
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/mdb_writer.py:63-105`, `:221-274` - one transaction per call, required / optional columns, INSERT text
- `ost_visualizer/application/services/project_write_service.py:698-777`, `:1171-1269` - `create_condition_result` (lock guard, overflow user message, uid kept when only the refresh fails)
- `ost_visualizer/domain/services/elevation.py:25-67` - `parse_elevation`, `reassemble_elevation` (' @T ' / ' @B ')
- `ost_visualizer/domain/entities/layer.py:68-102` - `is_shared_template_layer`, `merge_layers_for_bid`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/conditions_sidebar.py:243-264`, `:969-1001`, `:1499-1513` - "+" button, New > Condition, nearest folder ancestor as context

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_condition_operations.py` (`:407` cross-bid refusal; `:446` next UID = global MAX+1 and bid-scoped owners; `:487` root layer / folder and empty notes NULL; `ConditionInsertContractTests :968-1095`; overflow row `:1360-1416`; guard messages `:2133`); `tests/presentation/handlers/test_condition_action_handler.py` (`:7094` documented defaults, `:7182` default layer, `:7192` unknown type falls back, `:7598` per-type spec, `:5165`, `:5227`, `:5246`, `:7637`); `tests/domain/services/test_elevation.py:423-431`, `:27-31`; `tests/application/services/test_project_write_service.py:1355`, `:19668`. About 32 Windows tests port 1:1 or after a small Core extraction (estimate 60-75 host cases with data-safety additions); about 30 Qt / SQL / identity tests have no iOS counterpart. The real-dialog tests (`tests/integration/conditions/test_editor_navigation.py:556-762`) are the evidence for NC-1.

**iOS today / iOS files touched.** Today: `duplicate_condition` (allocator + guard model: `ProjectStore+Conditions.cpp:410-548`), form rules `Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.hpp:42-47`, style defaults `ConditionStyleDefaults.hpp:35-79`, `ConditionStyleChange.hpp:71-112`, `ConditionChoices.hpp:36-92`, the Edit dialog (`Controllers/EditConditionViewController/*`, `_applyChanges` `:359-398`), the Duplicate flow as the model for Create (`ViewController+Conditions.mm:119-270`, `ImportService.mm:437-480`). Missing in Core: the insert, `CreateConditionSpec`, the type defaults table, the default-layer rule, `reassemble_elevation`, create mode. `reassemble_elevation` exists today only as the Objective-C helper `reassembleElevation` (`Controllers/EditConditionViewController/Components/EditConditionHelpers.mm:92-103`, called at `EditConditionViewController.mm:616`; it trims only U+0020, Windows uses `value.strip()`, `elevation.py:62-67`), next to a second Objective-C copy of `parse_elevation` (`:51-90`) although Core has one (`PositionParser.cpp:1493`). Both move to Core in the same commit (below). New / changed files:
- NEW Core: `Core/Domain/ConditionCreate.hpp/.cpp` (`CreateConditionSpec`, `type_defaults_row`, `default_new_condition_layer`, `new_condition_form`, `build_create_spec`, `validate_create_spec`, `condition_create_failed_issue`; the 255-unit name limit of N-M1-13 is counted by S0's `ost_domain::utf16_length`, `Core/Domain/TextLimits.hpp`, 6.2 S0.b); `reassemble_elevation` in `PositionParser.*` (trims with `py_strip`); `folder_context_uid` lives in the folders module (5.2) (as built: it lives in `ConditionCreate.hpp/.cpp`, `folder_context_uid(nodes, index)` over the pre-order nodes of `build_condition_tree`, and is the ONE overload; the `(folder map, uid)` overload of `ConditionFolderRules` was removed, see 5.2)
- NEW store: `Components/ProjectStore+ConditionCreate.cpp` + `ProjectStore_ConditionCreate.hpp` (`insert_condition`); `allocate_condition_identities(count)` (internal; built in M1 for M2 cross-bid paste, 4.2; declared in `ProjectStore_ConditionCreate.hpp` so that M2 can call it)
- NEW Obj-C bridge `Services/ImportService+ConditionCreate.h/.mm`
- CHANGED UI: `Controllers/EditConditionViewController/*` (create mode: title New Condition, Save only, Cond. No. read-only, Name first responder, Save disabled while the write runs). Create mode has its own save path: `_applyChanges` exits early on `!_dirty` (`EditConditionViewController.mm:360`) and compares with `stored = _condition` for an unknown uid (:365-377), so an untouched Save would close the sheet and create nothing. In create mode Save therefore always runs `_validateAndCollectTextFields` (name rule "Condition name cannot be empty.", as Windows forces `_dirty = True`, `condition_action_handler.py:758`) with the Cond. No. field skipped (it is parsed today at :619-622), then calls a NEW delegate method `editConditionViewController:didRequestCreateCondition:failureMessage:` (as built: `editConditionViewController:didRequestCreateCondition:message:`, which answers an `EditConditionCreateOutcome`); the edit delegate `didSaveCondition:changes:` and the post-write `shift_conflicting_ref_nos` / `_conditionsMap[...] =` (:389-392) are not used. "Untouched" for the silent Cancel (N-M1-09) is a separate flag, not `_dirty`. Also changed: `EditConditionViewController.h` (delegate protocol), `EditConditionViewController_Internal.h`, `EditConditionHelpers.h/.mm` (`reassembleElevation` and the `parseElevation` copy are replaced by the Core functions), `Controllers/ConditionsViewController.h/.mm` ("+" bar button, delegate method, header hit-testing, see Touch adaptation), `ViewController+Conditions.mm` (present form, create delegate following Duplicate). Host tests cannot see this path, so the device pack carries the row M1-20 (6.4).
- NEW tests: `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_create.cpp`, `test_condition_create_form.cpp`

**Touch adaptation.** iPhone: the same `EditConditionViewController` in a create mode, full-height sheet; Cond. No. shown as "Next number: N"; Type and Layer are pickers of existing types and the bid's own layers (with "(none)"); Save disabled during the write so a double tap creates one condition; Cancel of an untouched form closes silently. Entry: "+" in the Conditions sheet bar creates in the folder of the selected condition, at the top level when none is selected (Windows `_on_new_clicked`, `conditions_sidebar.py:1392-1395`, `_resolve_folder_context_uid` :1499-1513); a long-press on a row OR a section header offers "New Condition in <folder>" (folder = `folder_uid` of the pressed section, root otherwise). The header press is new work: today `_handleLongPress:` uses `indexPathForRowAtPoint` and returns on nil (`ConditionsViewController.mm:353-355`), and an empty folder is a section with zero rows (:124-130), so header hit-testing through `rectForHeaderInSection:` is added (N-M1-14). iPad: centred form sheet; the seven pickers are popovers anchored on their rows (4.3, owner note m). After Save: row selected and revealed, no placement.

**Dependencies.** Lock guard (4.1), UID limit (4.2), popover fix (4.3); layers (5.3): the default-layer rule and picker use the same "bid's own layers" notion (the `Default` layer may be deleted); folders (5.2): folder context and folder ownership check; M3 export must read what iOS creates (Notes TEXT, flags 1 / 0, and the columns `Connect` / `ConnectTolerance` / `SnapToLinear`, which are Windows-schema columns, `database_creator.py:191-197`, exported by `ost_exporter.py:84-90`, that the Windows insert never writes).

**Risk.** Logic low (a small writer next to `duplicate_condition` (21 host tests in `test_condition_duplicate.cpp`; the sibling `update_condition` has 45 in `test_condition_update.cpp`); Windows tests run on SQLite). Persistence low-medium (Windows round trip reasoned, not run, until M3). UI medium (a create mode inside the ~870-line Edit dialog can regress Edit; iPad pickers; double tap; sheet / keyboard layout). Product decisions medium (N-M1-07 to N-M1-12).

**Size and order of work.** Core ~350 lines, tests ~900, UI ~250 (all figures are estimates). Order: (1) port the persistence tests red; (2) Core spec / defaults / reassemble tests red; (3) `insert_condition` + Core; (4) bridge; (5) create mode + "+" ; (6) device pack rows.

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register NC-1 to NC-12): NC-1 `edit_condition_dialog.py:858`, `:1480-1508` - the dialog shows Cond. No. "0", refuses it, then the typed number is dropped (MAX+1 wins) -> read-only next number (Windows d26893c fixed this too, `condition_action_handler.py:619-633`: the form shows MAX(ref_no) + 1, read-only; iOS shows the same number and, since parity pass 2, with no floor at 0: a bid whose numbers are all negative shows the writer's MAX+1, for example -4). NC-2 `condition_action_handler.py:547-551` - default layer picked from the MERGED list (shared template rows included); on a bid without its own "Default" every create fails -> default = the first layer of the merged list (`merged_layers`, list order) whose name is exactly "Default" AND which belongs to the bid (`bid_layers`), else none (Windows `:547-551` takes the first match of the merged list; a shared template row is skipped, `ConditionChoices.hpp:38-43`). The Core function takes the ordered `merged_layers` vector, not the map; its test ports `test_condition_action_handler.py:7182-7190`. NC-3 `condition_action_handler.py:758` - `_dirty` forced True, Cancel of an untouched form asks to save -> silent close. NC-6 `condition_action_handler.py:552-587` - a metric bid starts from imperial values -> metric defaults (N-M1-08). NC-9 `condition_operations.py:42-66` - UID unguarded at 2147483647 -> refuse. NC-4 name > 255 UTF-16 units after the suffix (`database_creator.py:166`), NC-8 CdnTypeUID existence unchecked (`condition_operations.py:326-334`), NC-10 non-finite numbers -> refused (N-M1-13). Not repeated here (see section 8): NC-5 (empty name accepted by the writer), NC-7 (type created before validation), NC-11 (no name uniqueness on create), NC-12 (locked bid refused silently). Windows d26893c also fixed NC-2, NC-3, NC-5, NC-6 and NC-10 (section 8, last column): iOS already behaved so, and its refusals of a blank name and of a non-finite number now carry the Windows texts ("Save Failed": "Condition name cannot be empty." / "The condition was not created because one of its values is not a finite number.").

**As built (M1).** Core `ConditionCreate.*` (spec, type defaults row built from `style_dimension_defaults` and `apply_style_change`, default layer, form, validation, failed-create issue) and `reassemble_elevation` in `PositionParser.*`; store `insert_condition` and `allocate_condition_identities` (`ProjectStore+ConditionCreate.cpp`); bridge `+[ImportService createCondition:inBid:error:]` (answers the new uid, nil on a refusal; nil with no text means "ask `isBidLocked:`"); UI: create mode of the Edit Condition form, "+" and "New Condition in <folder>" on rows and section headers, the read-only text "Next number: N" from `ost_domain::next_condition_number_text` (`ConditionFolderUi.*`, 64 bit). Differences from the text above: (1) the bid uid of `insert_condition` is read like Python `int()` (blanks, a sign and leading zeros accepted; zero, negative, non-numbers and values beyond 64 bits refused), where 4.4 would use the strict `integrity::parse_uid`; (2) the type uid is ASCII digits above zero (NC-8b); (3) a NUL, or notes or a name that are not valid UTF-8, are refused with the generic failure (the loader reads C strings); (4) `allocate_condition_identities` refuses `count < 1` and `count > 1,000,000` before building anything (the cap is the builder's); (5) the form notices typing through a `UIControlEventEditingChanged` action in create mode, so the swipe-down of an edited form asks to discard (`modalInPresentation` follows the dirty flag); the conformance to `UIAdaptivePresentationControllerDelegate` is public in `EditConditionViewController.h`; (6) a failed re-read after a committed create shows the Windows "Refresh Error" text. The Windows quirk COND-STYLE-02 is kept: a new Area made through the dialog gets the Spacing field value (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1292-1294`, `:1640`).

**Tests-first plan (M1).** Port 1:1 onto a SQLite `TempDb` (Conditions area, `snapshot_all`): next UID = global MAX+1 raised above dangling references, RefNo = MAX of the bid + 1, GUID shape, fixed columns (Arial, -16, ShowTakeoff -1 ...), every spec field in its own column, Backout -1 / 0, Notes NULL when empty, foreign layer / folder refused with 0 rows, missing required / optional columns, overflow text at 2147483647 with rows unchanged. Core-extracted: the synthetic-condition values, default-layer rule, per-type spec table, tuple = (thickness, spacing, shape, uom1, calc_type1, backout, display_grid_while_drawing) as asserted at `test_condition_action_handler.py:7619-7630` (`0:(4,4,-1,2,1,F,F) 1:(4,6,-1,5,11,F,T) 2:(4,4,0,0,23,T,F) 3:(0,4,0,0,23,T,F)`: only Area, type 1, draws its grid; Count and Attachment, types 2 and 3, have backout true), type 99 fallback, `reassemble_elevation` round trip. Data-safety (new): name of 256 UTF-16 units, and a name of 128 emoji (256 units, 128 characters) refused, 127 emoji accepted; empty / blank name, non-finite numbers, absent CdnType, UID at 2147483647, locked bid (0 rows), two calls = two rows, a `TempDb::block` trigger on the INSERT leaves 0 rows and an unchanged allocator; `allocate_condition_identities(3)` on a bid whose highest number is 2147483645 is refused whole with nothing written (the `refuse_new_condition_numbers(highest, count)` boundary), and `allocate_condition_identities(3)` gives consecutive numbers and distinct UIDs (if these two tests are not wanted in M1, the function moves to M2, 5.7).

---

### 5.2 Condition folders: create, rename, delete, move a condition (M, risk low-medium)

**What Windows does.** Exactly three folder writers exist: insert, rename, delete (`ost_visualizer/application/interfaces/i_mdb_writer.py:137-149`). There is NO folder move or reorder: `ParentUID` is written only at insert and nulled at delete; order is by name at display time. The only single-condition "move" is a CONDITION into a folder or the root (`update_condition` with `folder_uid`). The iOS `update_condition` exists but has no lock check (`ProjectStore+Conditions.cpp:198-386`), whereas Windows guards it (`project_write_service.py:1303`) and pins the move as a lock-blocked folder write (`tests/integration/permissions/test_toolbar_projection.py:464-499`); so "Move to Folder" gets its own store function (below) and never calls `update_condition`. A second Windows route moves several conditions: Cut + Paste in the sidebar onto a folder, the root or a type group (`conditions_sidebar.py:1569-1640`, `condition_action_handler.py:996-1179`; one `update_condition` per condition, `folder_uid` and, for a type group, `cdn_type_uid`; lock-blocked, `test_toolbar_projection.py:360-499`). It is NOT planned in M1: iOS offers the single-condition Move to Folder (N-M1-14), and Copy + Paste is the Duplicate that exists. Rules: a new folder is named "New Folder" (since Windows d26893c, `0a24294`: the first free sibling name "New Folder", "New Folder (2)", ..., compared stripped and case-insensitively inside the same parent, still created without asking); rename strips, refuses blank, does nothing when unchanged, refuses a case-insensitive sibling duplicate ("Duplicate Folder" / "Folder X already exists."); delete refuses a folder that holds a condition or has a descendant folder that does (service rule), texts "Delete N item(s)?" (since d26893c singular or plural: "Delete 1 item?", "Delete 3 items?"), "Cannot delete "X" because it is in use.", "Delete the other N item(s)?" (since d26893c "Delete the other 1 item?", "Delete the other 2 items?"); surviving sub-folders move to the ROOT; no undo, no mesh refresh.

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/condition_folder_operations.py:11-60`, `:62-85`, `:87-142` - insert (bid exists, parent in the same bid, allocator, `ExpandState=-1`), rename, delete (direct in-use check per 50-chunk `:100-123`, `ParentUID=NULL` for children `:124-131`)
- `ost_visualizer/application/services/project_write_service.py:779-822`, `:824-853`, `:866-928`, `:936-953` - orchestration, lock guard, THE in-use rule (ancestor walk, `:936-953`), blocked skipped / rest deleted; `invalidates_undo=False` is the default of `reload_conditions_and_notify`, `:1179`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/handlers/condition_action_handler.py:1367-1416`, `:1425-1455`, `:1514-1575`, `:1577-1617` - New Folder ("New Folder"), rename write-through, delete flow, move a condition to a folder
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/conditions_sidebar.py:39-131`, `:1455-1497`, `:1672-1716` - drag onto folder / root, rename validation (strip, blank, unchanged, sibling duplicate)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/utils/messagebox.py:78-119` - `confirm_multi_delete` texts
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/bulk_write_helpers.py:6`, `:22-101` (chunk 50); `identity_allocation.py:5-47`; `bid_owned_identity.py:105-235`, `:257-294`
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/database_creator.py:138-145` (`BidConditionFolders`: UID COUNTER, ParentUID INTEGER, Name VARCHAR(50), ExpandState YESNO)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/managers/ui_access_manager.py:69-236`, `:711-783` (`EDIT_CONDITION_STRUCTURE` gate); `application/condition_change_impact.py:27-82` (list refresh only)

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_condition_folder_operations.py` (14 persistence tests + 8 contract + 9 delete-safety + the real-Access scenario `test_folder_writes_scope_their_rows_and_roll_back_on_real_access`); `tests/integration/mdb/test_ownership_and_atomicity.py` (cross-bid batch, insert needs a bid, rollback at uid 51, orphan folder); `tests/application/services/test_project_write_service.py:16520-16552`, `:19220-19236` (F1 / F2 / F3 -> [F1, F2]) and rows for the in-use rule; `tests/presentation/components/test_conditions_sidebar.py:1246` ('  Renamed  ' -> 'Renamed'), `:1034`, `:1006`; `tests/integration/permissions/test_toolbar_projection.py` (locked bid disables every folder write). About 44 port 1:1 or with a small adaptation (estimate 45-60 host tests incl. data safety); about 40 more are orchestration / Qt / SQL-collaboration. The load side is already ported (22 tests: `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_folders.cpp`).

**iOS today / iOS files touched.** Read side: `Core/Domain/ConditionFolder.hpp` (cycle refusal), `ConditionTree.*`, `ProjectStore+Load.cpp:532-557`, `:815-823`, `update_condition` moving a condition (`ProjectStore+Conditions.cpp:198-386`, folder-in-bid check `:340`), the integrity helpers, `BrowserRules` (`normalize_folder_name`, project folders only), the alert-with-text-field pattern (`Controllers/DatabaseViewController.mm:234-380`). The Conditions list is flat sections without a folder uid (`ConditionsViewController.mm:22-26`, `:95-163`). New / changed:
- NEW Core: `Core/Domain/ConditionFolderRules.hpp/.cpp` (name check, whose 50-unit limit (N-M1-13) is counted by S0's `ost_domain::utf16_length`, rename decision, delete plan, texts, `folder_context_uid`, `effective_condition_folder_parent`) (as built: `check_condition_folder_name`, `decide_condition_folder_rename`, `plan_condition_folder_delete`, `condition_folder_delete_confirmation`, `effective_condition_folder_parent`; there is NO `folder_context_uid` in this module any more: the one rule is `ost_domain::folder_context_uid(nodes, index)` of `ConditionCreate.*` (5.1), because the `(folder map, uid)` overload of this module that the folders chain wrote had no caller and the New Condition chain's tree-node overload is the one the "+" button and the long press need; the UI layer adds `Core/Domain/ConditionFolderUi.*` (manager and picker rows, texts) and `ProjectStore_ConditionFolderAlerts.hpp` / `ProjectStore+ConditionFolderAlerts.cpp` (`condition_folder_failure_dialog`, the alert of every refusal or failure of a folder write))
- NEW store: `Components/ProjectStore+ConditionFolders.cpp` + `ProjectStore_ConditionFolders.hpp` (`create_condition_folder`, `rename_condition_folder`, `delete_condition_folders`, `move_condition_to_folder(db_path, bid_uid, condition_uid, folder_uid, std::string* error)` (bid lock 4.1, condition and folder belong to the bid, exactly one row, one transaction; "Move to Folder" calls it, never `update_condition`), result type with refusal enum) (as built: all four writers return a `ConditionFolderResult` (`ok`, `uid`, a `refusal` enum, `message`, `blocked`) and have no `std::string* error` parameter: `move_condition_to_folder(db_path, bid_uid, condition_uid, folder_uid)`; the screens word the refusals through `condition_folder_failure_dialog`)
- `Core/Domain/ConditionTree.cpp`: make `emit_children` iterative (an 8000-deep chain is read by Windows; the recursive build can overflow a 1 MB main-thread stack)
- NEW bridge `Services/ImportService+ConditionFolders.h/.mm`
- NEW UI `Controllers/ConditionFoldersViewController.h/.mm` (folder manager and folder picker); CHANGED `ConditionsViewController.h/.mm` ("Folders" button, `folder_uid` per section, "Move to Folder..." in the row long-press sheet), `ViewController+Conditions.mm` (reload conditions only)
- NEW tests: `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_folder_rules.cpp`, `test_condition_folder_write.cpp`, `test_condition_folder_delete.cpp`

**Touch adaptation.** iPhone: a "Folders" button in the Conditions navigation bar pushes a folder manager (indented tree with direct condition counts); "+" = New Folder at top level; swipe left = Delete / Rename; swipe right or context menu = New Folder Inside; edit mode = multi-select delete. The same screen in picker mode ("Move to Folder", "(No folder)" row, current folder checked) replaces drag and drop and opens from the condition long-press sheet. Create and rename use a `UIAlertController` with a text field (New Folder prefilled with the first free sibling name, "New Folder", "New Folder (2)", ... (parity pass 2; the first build prefilled the constant "New Folder"), Create / Cancel; Cancel creates nothing). Delete uses the Windows texts plus one iOS line that sub-folders move to the top level. iPad: the same pushes inside the existing page sheet; any action sheet is anchored. Locked bid: every blocked tap (the Conditions "+" and "New Condition in <folder>", the folder manager's actions, "Move to Folder...") first calls S0's `_refuseIfBidLocked` (4.1; the folder manager is not a `ViewController` category and reaches it through its delegate, implemented in `ViewController+Conditions.mm`), which shows the lock alert; the store re-checks. After a write only the Conditions list reloads.

**Dependencies.** Lock guard (4.1); UID limit (4.2: applied to folders too, N-M1-04); New Condition (5.1) passes a `folder_uid`, so `insert_condition` verifies the folder belongs to the bid (`condition_operations.py:271-279`); shared files: `ConditionsViewController` and `ViewController+Conditions.mm` (one owner, section 6). Later: M2 paste clears the folder on a cross-bid paste; M3 export writes `BidConditionFolders` with ExpandState (`ost_exporter.py:209-216`) and the CSV sorts the folder path with the summary rule (register CF-9).

**Risk.** Low-medium. Core is small and heavily pinned. Product decisions medium (N-M1-14 to N-M1-17; N-M1-04 for the UID limit). Medium: a NEW screen (the flat list carries no folder uids); alert-with-text-field has a precedent, but the Objective-C is compile-checked only until the device pack; the lock reader reads `JobStatuses`, which iOS never used for locking. Low: iterative traversal, name length, allocator. Windows behaviours not run: the Access failure for names over 50 characters and Python recursion on deep chains (inferred from reading).

**Size and order of work.** M (all figures are estimates): Core ~600 lines WITHOUT the lock reader (built by S0, 4.1 / 6.1; re-derive the figure when the plan is approved), 45-60 host tests, Objective-C ~400 lines (one screen). Order: (1) in-use rule and name rule tests red; (2) store tests red (14 + 8 + 9 + scenario); (3) Core rules + store; (4) iterative tree + 8000-deep test; (5) bridge; (6) manager screen + list integration; (7) device pack rows.

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register CF-1 to CF-12): CF-1 `condition_folder_operations.py:106-123` vs `project_write_service.py:936-953` - the Access layer blocks only DIRECT use, the service blocks every ancestor; the app calls the service path -> the store refuses when the folder or any descendant holds a condition, evaluated from rows inside the transaction (the Access-layer scenario expectation is flipped). CF-3 `condition_folder_operations.py:124-131` - sub-folders go to the TOP level and the confirmation does not say so -> kept, and said. CF-5 `condition_action_handler.py:1389-1409` vs `conditions_sidebar.py:1472-1479` - New Folder allows duplicates, rename refuses -> sibling rule at create (name first). Windows d26893c (`condition_action_handler.py:1336-1350`, `_free_folder_name`) now creates at once with the first free name, so duplicates no longer arise there; iOS keeps name-first and prefills that same free name (converged except that iOS asks). CF-6 name > 50 (`database_creator.py:142`) -> refuse. CF-7 failures only logged (`condition_action_handler.py:1410-1412`) -> alert with the DB-locked hint. Windows d26893c (`:1321-1334`, `_report_folder_write_failure`) now alerts too: "Failed to create the condition folder." / "Failed to rename the condition folder." / "Failed to delete the condition folder." / "Failed to delete N condition folders." plus the DB-locked hint (silent for a locked bid); iOS shows these sentences under its own titles (New Folder, Rename Folder, Delete Folder) since parity pass 2 and says "This bid is locked." for a locked bid. Not repeated here (see section 8): CF-2 (in-use check reads memory), CF-4 (rename / delete not bound to the open bid), CF-8, CF-9, CF-10, CF-11, CF-12.

**As built (M1).** (1) The batch delete of the store is all or nothing (`InUse`, with `blocked` in request order), where the Windows service skips the blocked folders and deletes the rest (`project_write_service.py:876-893`); the screen calls `plan_condition_folder_delete` first and sends only the deletable folders. The in-use rule counts the conditions of ANY bid (a safe superset of CF-1) and is exact to Windows for a requested uid that is no folder (a dangling `ParentUID`; 13 oracle rows from the real function). (2) A rename to the stored name (after the strip) is a success that writes nothing. (3) The folder manager is `Controllers/ConditionFoldersViewController.*`: the indented tree with direct counts, swipe left Delete / Rename, swipe right New Folder Inside, the context menu (its alerts are presented on the next run-loop turn, like the swipe actions), Edit = multi-select delete; the same class is the "Move to Folder" picker ("(No folder)", the current folder checked); a second tap on "Folders" while the manager is on top does nothing. (4) "Move to Folder" re-selects only `selectedConditionUID` in the list. (5) Texts that are iOS-only: "Folder name must not be longer than 50 characters.", the delete line "Folders inside it move to the top level.", and, for a failed re-read after a committed folder write (CF-8), the title "Refresh Error" with the Windows sentence of the action done (parity pass 2, Windows d26893c: "The folder was created", "The folder was renamed", "The folder was deleted" / "The folders were deleted" or "The condition was moved", then ", but the conditions list could not be refreshed. Reopen the database to see the latest conditions."; `condition_folder_refresh_failed_text`, wired in `ViewController+Conditions.mm`; the first build had the iOS-only "The folder change was saved, but the list could not be refreshed. Reopen the bid to see it."). The confirmation "Move this bid to Deleted Bids?" lost its single quotes in the same pass (Windows `d2caf63`). (6) A folder that holds only sub-folders has no section, so the item "New Condition in" a folder is not offered for such a folder (open point 9a.7). CF-10 is closed by an iterative `emit_children` and a test with a chain of 8000 folders.

**Tests-first plan (M1).** Port the 14 persistence + 8 contract + 7 of 9 delete-safety tests (the log-text test `:631` and the Access chunk-size test `:725` do not port) and the ownership / atomicity parts onto `TempDb`; flip the real-Access scenario (deleting Mid must now be refused because Leaf holds a condition); the in-use plan as pure Domain tests ([F1, F2] example, request order kept, unknown folder blocks nothing); the rename rule as a pure function ('  Renamed  ' -> 'Renamed', blank / unchanged silent, 'other' vs 'Other' sibling -> one "Duplicate Folder"). Data safety (new): name of 51 UTF-16 units, blank name, parent of another bid, 120-folder batches with a used or foreign folder at every chunk edge (rows untouched), `TempDb::block` rollback of the ParentUID update, 8000-deep chain loads and shows, lock refusal writes nothing; `move_condition_to_folder`: a locked bid refuses the move (`snapshot_all` unchanged) and a foreign folder is refused.

---

### 5.3 Layers: create, rename, delete, reorder (M, risk medium-low)

**What Windows does.** Layers are managed in three UIs (main-window Layers sidebar, the Layers dialog from Edit Condition, the Default Layers dialog) over the same writers in `layer_operations.py`, each in ONE transaction. New layer: Show -1, IsTemplate 0, IsLocked 0, Sequence = after_sequence+1, the bid's rows with a greater Sequence shift +1, UID = MAX+1 raised above dangling `BidLayerUID` references. Rename and delete touch only own unlocked non-template rows (SQL guard `IsTemplate=0 AND IsLocked=0`); delete first sets `BidLayerUID` NULL in 11 reference tables, deletes, then renumbers the later rows of the same bid. Reorder = a swap of the Sequence values of the selected row and its neighbour in the merged list. Name rules (strip, blank ignored, duplicate by strip + lower against the listed rows, text "Layer {name} already exists.") and the in-use rule ('Cannot delete "{name}" because it is in use.') live only in the UI.

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/layer_operations.py:78-114` (insert), `:153-217` (delete), `:385-410` (rename), `:432-465` (swap); default-layer twins `:57-76`, `:116-151`, `:219-257`
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/identity_allocation.py:5-39`; `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/database/bid_owned_identity.py:105-152`, `:205-235`
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/constants.py:122-134` - `LAYER_REFERENCE_TABLES` (the 11 tables)
- `ost_visualizer/domain/entities/layer.py:4-118` - reserved names, `normalize_layer_name`, `is_shared_template_layer`, `merge_layers_for_bid`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/layers_dialog.py:285-306`, `:315-480`, `:487-548`, `:628-684` - UI rules (`_find_layer_by_name :293-298`), after_sequence, delete flow, move
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/layers_sidebar.py:196-272`, `:294-354`, `:416-453`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/utils/messagebox.py:78-119` - `confirm_multi_delete`
- `ost_visualizer/application/services/project_write_service.py:6607-6642`, `:6670-6725`, `:6797-6815`, `:6832-6854` - lock guard (`:6616`), write then reload, partial batch delete, rename then `ost_visualizer/application/layer_change_impact.py:12-34` (`layer_rename_preserves_rendering`: a rename touching annotation / comments / image refreshes images and meshes, any other rename only the lists; pure port of `tests/application/test_layer_change_impact.py`, 7 tests)

- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/database_creator.py:306-314` (BidLayers: Name VARCHAR(50), Sequence INTEGER)

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_layer_operations.py` (insert 4 tests: dangling reference UID '9' / '5' reserved, only later rows of that bid shift, missing bid refused, failed shift leaves nothing; delete 9; rename 3; swap 6; real-Access lifecycle scenario `:2226-2530` as ONE SQLite scenario); `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_identity_allocation.py` (8 portable BidLayers allocator tests: `:110`, `:122`, `:134`, `:142`, `:178`, `:185`, `:196`, `:204`; `:268` pins the Windows schema-inspector call order and is not ported); `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_settings_reader.py` (in-use reader: 3 data cases); UI rules as pure Core: `tests/presentation/dialogs/test_layers_dialog.py`, `test_layers_sidebar.py`, `tests/integration/visibility/test_layer_delete_usage.py`. About 38 core tests (estimate 55-65 host cases with data safety). Deliberately NOT ported: `:1122` (dead lenient branch, LY-5), settings_reader `:850` (fail-open, LY-2), in-memory in-use tests `:1020`, `:2876` (LY-3), partial batch delete tests (LY-4). Not ported (engineering choice, no owner decision: iOS always has the full BidLayers schema): the legacy-schema / missing-column tests (about a dozen: `:859`, `:1106`, `:1159`, `:1169`, `:744`, `:2038`, `:1611-1634` and the SQL-text pins). Optional only if N-M1-18 = yes: the 27 default-layer tests.

**iOS today / iOS files touched.** Read and visibility half exists: `Core/Domain/Layer.hpp:11-92`, `LayerMerge.cpp:19-132`, `ProjectStore+Load.cpp:205-262`, `:487-510`, `ProjectStore+Takeoffs.cpp:40-130`, `OstSchema.cpp:295-303`, `:983-988`, `:817-863`, `:912`, `:931` (the 11 `BidLayerUID` entries of `FK_INDEXES`; the 11 relationship rows are `OstSchema.cpp:1172-1277`), `ConditionChoices.cpp:38-52`, the Layers sheet (`ViewController+Layers.mm:9-74`, `:252-320`), `ImportService.mm:353-370`. New / changed:
- NEW Core: `Core/Domain/LayerEdit.hpp/.cpp` (name check with verdicts: its 50-unit limit is counted by S0's `ost_domain::utf16_length` and its control-character / line-break refusal is S0's `ost_domain::has_control_character`, `Core/Domain/TextLimits.hpp`, N-M1-13; `new_layer_after_sequence`, move plan, delete plan with the Windows texts, limits)
- NEW store: `Components/ProjectStore+Layers.cpp` + `ProjectStore_Layers.hpp` (`insert_layer`, `rename_layer`, `delete_layers` atomic, `swap_layer_sequence`, `read_layer_uids_in_use`, `reload_layers(db_path, bid_uid, own_layers, merged_layers, error*)`, which refills BOTH layer structures of `ProjectData` the way `load_project` does: `ProjectData::layers` (the bid's own layers by uid with `visible` and `sequence`: the `SELECT UID, Name, Show, Sequence FROM BidLayers WHERE BidUID = ?` inline at `ProjectStore+Load.cpp:490-510`) and `ProjectData::merged_layers` (`read_layer_list`)); `ProjectStore+Load.cpp` moves that query and `read_layer_list` out of its anonymous namespace and exports both (as built: it exports `ost_store::layer_reads::read_merged_layers` and `read_own_layers`, both with an error text, declared in `ProjectStore_Layers.hpp`, and keeps `read_layer_list` file-private as a thin wrapper) (a `reload_layers` that returns only the merged list leaves `ProjectData::layers` stale: `condition_layer_choices(merged_layers, layers)` at `EditConditionViewController.mm:117` then drops a new layer from the picker, and `is_condition_visible`, `is_listed_layer_visible` and the visibility toggle at `ViewController+Layers.mm:307-309` ignore a uid that `layers` does not hold); S2 assigns both results into `mutableProject`
- NEW bridge `Services/ImportService+Layers.h/.mm` (no `ProjectService` edit: `sortedLayerUIDs` is already a mutable accessor, `ProjectService.h:24`; the rebuild from `merged_layers` is a helper in `ViewController+LayerEditing.mm`, as `_handleLayerToggle` does at `ViewController+Layers.mm:16-19`)
- NEW UI `Controllers/ViewController/Components/ViewController+LayerEditing.mm`; CHANGED `ViewController+Layers.mm` (nav bar "+", swipe, context menu; `_handleLayerToggle` also opens the sheet for an empty merged list); the Conditions list is reloaded through the existing `_refreshConditionsListForLayerVisibility` (`ViewController+Layers.mm:128-135`, which calls `loadConditionsFromProject:`); that method is defined only inside `ViewController+Layers.mm` and no header declares it, so S2 adds its declaration to S2's marker block `M1 layers (S2)` of `ViewController_Internal.h` before `ViewController+LayerEditing.mm` calls it, and in the same block every method of `ViewController+LayerEditing.mm` that `ViewController+Layers.mm` calls or names in `@selector(...)` for the nav bar "+", the swipe actions and the context menu (an undeclared call is a compile error under ARC, an undeclared `@selector` adds a first-party `-Wundeclared-selector` warning, 6.1); `ConditionsViewController.mm` is not edited (S1 owns it, 6.1)
- NEW tests: `Config/Tests/Persistence/test_layer_edit_store.cpp`, `test_layer_edit_rules.cpp`; the BidLayers allocator cases go into `test_layer_edit_store.cpp` (`test_uid_allocation.cpp` belongs to S0, 6.1)

**Touch adaptation.** Tap on a row already toggles visibility, so the Windows select-then-press model does not map. iPhone and iPad (same page sheet with medium / large detents): "+" in the nav bar = New Layer at the end (= Windows with no selection; `_handleLayerToggle` must also open the sheet when `merged_layers` is empty, because Windows allows New on an empty list (`test_layers_sidebar.py:221-246`, `after_sequence` 0) and today it returns at `ViewController+Layers.mm:13-15`); trailing swipe actions Delete (destructive) and Rename; long-press menu per row: Rename, New Layer Below, Move Up, Move Down, Delete (items Windows disables are hidden or disabled: locked copies, shared rows, list ends, shared neighbour). Names by `UIAlertController` (alert style, one text field, action disabled while the stripped text is blank); duplicate / refused names show the Windows text; a write that committed but whose `reload_layers` failed keeps the new state and shows the Windows text (create: `Refresh Error` / `The layer was created, but the layer list could not be refreshed. Reopen the database to see the new layer.`; delete: `Delete Layer` / `Layers were deleted, but the refresh failed.`; rename and move, since parity pass 2 (the Windows d26893c sentences; titles `Rename Layer` / `Move Layer` since job 11, owner decision 2, `Refresh Error` before): `Rename Layer` | `Move Layer` / `The layer was renamed|moved, but the layer list could not be refreshed. Reopen the database to see the change.`); other failures: `New Layer` / `Failed to create layer.`, `Rename Layer` / `Failed to rename layer.`, `Move Layer` / `Failed to move layer.`, `Delete Layer` / `Failed to delete layer.`, with no DB-locked hint since job 11 (owner decision 2: the Windows dialog never has it; N-M1-17 applies to the other iOS-worded failures only). Delete = one layer per action with the Windows confirm or in-use text, usage read when the action starts (unreadable = refuse). Reorder = adjacent swap only, no drag handles. No action sheet without an anchor. Locked bid: every write action ("+", Rename, New Layer Below, Move Up / Down, Delete) first calls S0's `_refuseIfBidLocked` (4.1); the store re-checks.

**Dependencies.** Lock guard, UID limit; New Condition and the Edit Condition layer picker (`EditConditionViewController+Pickers.mm:112-146`) consume the layer list and the in-use rule (a created layer is offered at once, a deleted one never stays referenced); folders, Set Scale, page rename / delete are independent; page delete does not touch layers. M3: export of `BidLayers` needs 32-bit UID / Sequence and names within 50 units. M4: the in-use read already covers annotation and hotlink tables.

**Risk.** Medium-low. SQL is small and strongly pinned (~40 portable tests), but: tap-to-toggle vs selection (swipe + menu mitigate); C++ signed overflow at 2147483647 (`BidLayer::sequence` is `int`, `column_int` truncates: use `long long`, check before arithmetic); stale memory after writes (`sortedLayerUIDs`, conditions' layer names, Edit Condition copies: `reload_layers` after every write, delete included; never `_loadProject:`, which clears the undo history and refits the camera, `ViewController.mm:1388-1407`); fail-closed rules Windows lacks need clear generic messages; tied or gapped Sequence makes Move look like a no-op (kept); UIKit paths are compile-checked only; the Layers sheet and `ProjectData` are shared with parallel features.

**Size and order of work.** M. Order: (1) allocator / insert / delete / rename / swap tests red; (2) rule tests (name verdicts, positions, delete plan); (3) `LayerEdit` + store; (4) bridge + the `sortedLayerUIDs` rebuild helper; (5) sheet UI; (6) device pack rows.

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register LY-1 to LY-14; this paragraph repeats the rows that change the build, the others, LY-5, LY-6 and LY-10 to LY-14, are in section 8): LY-1 `layers_dialog.py:293-298`, `:403-407` - the duplicate check uses the LISTED rows, which never include the Comments row, so "Comments" can be created -> an invisible, undeletable layer -> "comments" is always taken (duplicate message). LY-2 `project_read_service.py:122-127` - a failed usage read returns an EMPTY set, so every layer looks unused and delete then clears references -> unreadable usage refuses the delete. LY-3 `settings_reader.py:450-476` vs `ost_visualizer/domain/services/project_data_service.py:560-572` - two definitions of "in use" -> the 11-table rule everywhere. LY-4 `project_write_service.py:6689-6725` - batch delete commits the earlier deletes when the guard refuses a later one -> one transaction, all or nothing. LY-7 `identity_allocation.py:5-8`, `layer_operations.py:88-97`, `:211-216` - no 2147483647 guard on UID / Sequence -> refuse before any write. LY-8 name > 50 (`database_creator.py:310`) -> refuse (N-M1-13). LY-9 `layer_operations.py:153-217`, `:385-410`, `:432-465` via `bid_owned_identity.py:205-235` - layer checked against ONE bid, not the OPEN bid -> bid argument.

**As built (M1).** Bridge `ImportService+Layers`: `+insertLayerNamed:afterSequence:inBid:error:`, `+renameLayer:toName:inBid:error:`, `+deleteLayers:inBid:`, `+swapLayer:withLayer:inBid:`, `+layerUIDsInUse:inBid:` (NO = unreadable, the caller must refuse the delete) and `+reloadLayersForBid:ownLayers:mergedLayers:error:`; every string reaches Core with its exact bytes, so a NUL is refused by the store and not cut. The UI adds Core `LayerSheet.*` (row actions, failure texts) and `LayerChangeImpact.*` (`layer_rename_preserves_rendering`, a pure port of `tests/application/test_layer_change_impact.py`). Differences from the text above: (1) the `delete_layers` batch takes every uid through `parse_uid`, so an empty or malformed uid in the list refuses the whole delete (LY-4b: Windows silently drops empty uids and keeps "7" and "007" as two); (2) all six layer functions return their negative result on an exception (function try-blocks, commit `17b3a1a`), found by the whole-milestone review and proven with an allocation-failure probe that is not committed; (3) the name alert accepts Return, selects the old name when it opens for a rename (`selectedTextRange` on the next run-loop turn, not `selectAll:`), and has no auto-correction; (4) after a successful delete the page is redrawn (a hidden "Image" layer changes whether the PDF shows); (5) the four generic failure texts (create, rename, move, delete) carried the DB-locked hint in M1 (removed in job 11, owner decision 2), and a change that committed but whose re-read failed shows a "Refresh Error" alert (create only since job 11: rename and move are titled "Rename Layer" / "Move Layer"; rename and move: the Windows sentences "The layer was renamed|moved, but the layer list could not be refreshed. Reopen the database to see the change." since parity pass 2, where the first build had the iOS-only "The change was saved, but the layer list could not be refreshed. Reopen the database to see it."; create and delete use the Windows texts named above; the CF-8 twin); (6) the Layers sheet opens one at a time (`if (self.presentedViewController) return;`); (7) after a delete nothing is selected (no selection model), after create, rename and move the row is selected and scrolled into view; (8) parity pass 2 (Windows d26893c): Show All / Hide All in a bid with no layer of its own (only shared rows or none) shows "Layer Visibility" / "This bid has no layers of its own to show or hide." (it was silent; closes owner note c of job 6; a Hide All when every own layer is already hidden stays silent), and the delete confirmation reads "Delete 1 item?" / "Delete 3 items?"; (9) parity pass 3 (Windows d1c430a, owner note b): a SINGLE tap on a locked template layer that other bids share (IsTemplate and IsLocked and the layer's bid is not this bid, no exemption for Image) is refused with "Layer Visibility" / "This layer is a locked template layer that other bids share, so it cannot be shown or hidden from this bid.", nothing is written and the sheet reloads so that the check mark returns (before: the toggle went to the store and the generic "Failed to update layer visibility." appeared); the store (`ost_store::set_layer_visibility`) also refuses such a row as a fail-closed guard that Windows lacks (Hide All / Show All use another function and are unchanged); register row LY-15. Where Windows keeps the typed text after a refused name, iOS closes the alert first: open point 9a.7.

**Tests-first plan (M1).** Port 1:1 (use `TempDb::block` for the failed-step cases): insert 4, delete 9, rename 3 (the duplicate-physical-UID half of `:752` is dropped, the shared-row half is kept), swap 5 (`:1962` is dropped: `BidLayers.UID` is `INTEGER PRIMARY KEY`, `OstSchema.cpp:296`), allocator 8, in-use 3, the lifecycle scenario (rename with quotes; insert after 1 gives 101:1, New:2, 102:3, 103:4, 104:5; swap 101 / 103; a failing renumber rolls back the delete and the reference clearing; delete 102 clears only its references and renumbers 101:3, 104:4); UI rules as Core tests (strip, blank ignored, duplicate text, `after_sequence` 0 for an empty list / selected sequence / max sequence, editable only when not template and not locked, mixed selection sends only deletable rows). Data safety (new): UID and Sequence at 2147483647 (nothing written, no half shift), name of 51 units, "Comments" / "comments " refused, control characters and line breaks refused, layer of another bid refused, unreadable usage refuses, a layer referenced in each of the 11 tables in turn: the store keeps the Windows contract (the reference is cleared in that table only, other layers' and other bids' rows intact: `test_layer_operations.py:145`, `:1064`); the REFUSAL is the usage read plus the delete plan (`read_layer_uids_in_use` counts each of the 11 tables, the `LayerEdit` delete plan answers "in use", an unreadable read refuses), tested as one chain and not inside `delete_layers`.

### 5.4 Set Scale (L, risk HIGH for data, medium for UI)

**What Windows does.** Set Scale is a RATIO editor, not a calibrate-from-measured-length tool: a style (1 Architectural, 2 Civil, 3 Metric) with a preset or a custom first : second pair, plus an optional "apply to all pages". Saving multiplies every stored coordinate of the page (the 17 position tables, one of them the XML legends; the overlay rect and its offsets) by `new_ratio / old_ratio` (ratio = `ScaleFactor2 / ScaleFactor1`; positions are real-world inches, no unit choice), then writes only `ScaleFactor1` / `ScaleFactor2`, all in ONE transaction; sheet positions stay, quantities change. No undo entry of its own, but undo entries of other actions rescale on replay through captured page scales. No Windows test pins the presets, the 'Invalid Scale' validation texts or the `{:g}` formatting (source only); only `Save Failed` / `Failed to save page scale.` is pinned (`tests/presentation/dialogs/test_set_scale_dialog.py:49-50`, `tests/integration/pages/test_set_scale_apply.py:263`).

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/set_scale_dialog.py:10-357` - style combo, predefined / custom, apply-to-all, dirty tracking, texts, `{:g}` fill (`:149-150`, `:348-350`), validation (`:246-262`: `float(text)`, `value <= 0`; nan / inf pass)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/utils/scales.py:4-74` - preset tables (12 Arch, 11 Civil, 17 Metric, `ALL_SCALES` 40), `format_custom_scale`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/page_settings_bar.py:38-98`, `:159-206`, `:430-451` - bar combo, first-match preset (1e-9)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/ui_event_coordinator.py:5168-5208`, `:5381-5409`, `:5493-5665` - wiring, `_save_scale_settings`, preconditions
- `ost_visualizer/application/services/project_write_service.py:6306-6339`, `:6371-6433` - `save_page_scale(s)`, unique non-empty uids
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/page_operations.py:28-217` - scope check, stored sf, factor, overlay rect rewrite, 17 tables in fixed order, final UPDATE, one transaction
- `ost_visualizer/domain/services/page_scale_transform.py:1-68`; `domain/entities/overlay.py:7-19` (the validity rule of the DB path)
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/serialization.py:18-51`, `legend_position.py:6-26`, `overlay_rect.py:17-48` - position text forms (annotation tables exact repr; takeoffs / comments / typgroup 3-decimal trimmed), legend dX / dY, rect `%.6f`; `ost_visualizer/domain/entities/annotation.py:20-27` (`annotation_rotation_index`) and `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/database/annotation_storage.py:18-40` (`ANNOTATION_TYPE_BY_TABLE`, the 13 exact-value tables) decide which index keeps its original value

- `ost_visualizer/presentation/managers/ui_access_manager.py:711-741` - `EDIT_PAGE_SETTINGS` gate

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/domain/services/test_page_scale_transform.py` (factor (0.125,12)->(0.1875,12) = 2/3; (0,12)->(0.25,12) = 48; 1e-9 epsilon; rotation kept over 20 round trips; ported 1:1 against `position_rescale_factor_between_page_scales`, 4.10); `tests/presentation/utils/test_scales.py` (labels, `.15g`); `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_page_operations.py` (scale parts: per-page rewrite, overlay rect / offsets, malformed rect refused before any write, epsilon exclusive, rollback on a broken legend, text forms `0.5;1.0;1.5;2.0` vs `0.5;1;1.5;2`); `test_legend_position.py` (3-decimal trim table: 1.23456 -> 1.235, 1234.567 x 0.3 -> 370.37); `tests/domain/services/test_project_data_service.py` (memory: (1,96)->(1,48): [96,192,288,384] -> [48,96,144,192]); `tests/integration/pages/test_set_scale_apply.py`; `tests/presentation/handlers/test_plan_view_action_handler.py:3849` (undo after scale change: [0,0,96,0] -> [0,0,64,0]); `tests/integration/geometry/test_overlay_storage_calibration.py` (units 64 / 96 / 50). Also: `tests/integration/persistence/test_page_scale_geometry.py`, `tests/integration/geometry/test_page_scale_projection.py:19-41` (a rescaled takeoff keeps its sheet position), `tests/presentation/components/test_page_settings_bar.py` (`:170`, `:196`, `:209`, `:295`), `tests/application/services/test_project_write_service.py` (`:781`, `:836`, `:873-904`, `:906`), `tests/presentation/coordinators/test_ui_event_coordinator.py` (`:12565`, `:12594`, `:30469-30503`). 95 Windows tests are portable (estimate 110-130 host cases).

**iOS today / iOS files touched.** Read side only: `Core/Domain/Page.hpp:15-16`, loader `ProjectStore+Load.cpp:411-452` (0 coalesced to 1.0 at `:429-432`), `ProjectData.hpp:79-87` (`page_scale_ratio`), `Core/Domain/PositionParser.hpp` (`parse_position`, `python_float_repr`), `ProjectStore+Takeoffs.cpp:176-278` (write pattern), `ProjectStore_Internal.hpp:107-112` (`serialize_position`), `UndoRedoController.mm:7-20` (no page scale), `ViewController.mm:292-365` (toolbar), `ViewController+Conditions.mm:119-155` (form sheet). New / changed:
- NEW Core: `Core/Domain/PageScale.hpp/.cpp` (presets, matching, labels), `PageScaleRules.hpp/.cpp` (form validation with the Windows texts, session state), `PageScaleTransform.hpp/.cpp` (4.10: `units_per_sheet_inch`, `position_rescale_factor_between_page_scales`, `rescale_position_text`, `rescale_legend_xml`, `rescale_overlay_rect`, `rescale_entry_positions`, and `annotation_rotation_index`: `rescale_position_text` keeps the rotation slot of the 13 annotation tables from day one, `annotation.py:20-27`; M4's `Annotation.hpp` calls it, no second copy), `Core/Domain/OverlayRect.hpp/.cpp` (`parse_rect_storage`, `serialize_rect_storage`: `%.6f`, `overlay_rect.py:33-48`; created here, extended by M4, 5.15); `format_g` is added to `NumberText.*` with the other Python formatters (4.6, 6.1)
- NEW store: `Components/ProjectStore+PageScale.cpp` + `ProjectStore_PageScale.hpp` (`save_page_scales(db_path, bid_uid, page_uids, sf1, sf2, outcome*)` all-or-nothing, `save_page_scale` wrapper); `ProjectData::apply_page_scales` memory twin (as built: the free function `ost_domain::apply_page_scales(pages, takeoffs, uids, sf1, sf2)` of `PageScaleTransform.*`, which answers the changed page uids; there is no `ProjectData` member) written like the STORE, not like Windows `project_data_service.py:358-418`: takeoff positions rounded to 3 decimals (`serialization.py:26-27`), overlay rect text and BOTH offsets taken from the scaled rect (`page_operations.py:112-118`), so memory == reload (agreement test). Of the 9 memory oracles 7 port 1:1; `test_project_data_service.py:2298` is adapted (offsets expected (5, 10), not Windows (2, 3)) and `:2380` becomes "a negative-extent rect is refused, nothing changes" (same as the store)
- CHANGED undo: `UndoRedoController.{h,mm}` (a captured `PageScale` per page of each entry in the file-local `UndoEntry`, replay through `position_rescale_factor_between_page_scales`). The controller has no project access (`UndoRedoController.h` imports only Core types, `:5-7`; its push methods take uids, positions and takeoffs, `:55-77`; the `UndoRedoDelegate` protocol has no page-scale accessor, `:22-47`), so the pairs come through TWO new `@optional` methods of the `UndoRedoDelegate` protocol, declared in `UndoRedoController.h`: `undoRedoPageScaleForTakeoff:(const std::string &)uid pageUID:(std::string &)pageUID` (called only when an entry is created, while the takeoff still exists: it fills the page uid and answers the (`ScaleFactor1`, `ScaleFactor2`) pair of that page, or "unknown") and `undoRedoPageScaleForPage:(const std::string &)pageUID` (called at replay for the CURRENT pair of a page uid that the entry stored, or "unknown"; S4 fixes the remaining signature details, for example how the pair is returned). The entry stores, per takeoff, the page uid and the captured pair (a Place and a Delete already hold `Takeoff::page_uid` in the entry; for a Delete the creation-time call runs before the delegate removes the takeoffs, like the snapshot). At replay the controller never looks a takeoff up in the project, so the undo of a Delete and the redo of a Place, where the takeoff is absent from `ProjectData` (`UndoRedoController.mm:47-108` re-adds it from the entry), rescale correctly, like Windows, which keys the lookup by PAGE uid (`plan_view_action_handler.py:1706-1725` `_capture_item_page_scales`, `:1788` `_page_scale_for_page_uid(spec.page_uid)`; the pinned scenario is `test_plan_view_action_handler.py:3849`, a delete undone after a scale change). A delegate without the methods, or a page that is unknown at CREATION time, means nothing is captured for that takeoff and its replay factor is 1 (no rescale; NOT the captured pair (1.0, 1.0), which `position_rescale_factor_between_page_scales` would turn into the current ratio, 48 on a 1/4 inch sheet); a page that is unknown at REPLAY makes that undo / redo refuse (the apply method answers NO, the history does not advance, UND-02) instead of using the pair (1.0, 1.0) that the Windows lookup answers for an unknown page (`_page_scale_for_page_uid`, `:1667-1673`). The methods are implemented in S4's own category `ViewController+PageScale.mm` (a category may implement the methods of a protocol that the class extension already adopts, `ViewController.mm:22-23`), so `ViewController.mm` (the only `UndoRedoDelegate` implementation, `:974-1019`, `:1289`) and its push call sites (`:605`, `:1220`, `:1224`, `:1335`) are NOT edited by S4 (6.1). The pure replay helper `rescale_entry_positions(positions, captured, current)` goes into `PageScaleTransform.hpp`; `UndoHistory.hpp` is not changed (a missing capture means nothing captured and a replay factor of 1)
- NEW bridge `Services/ImportService+PageScale.h/.mm`; NEW UI `Controllers/PageScaleViewController/PageScaleViewController.{h,mm}`, `ViewController+PageScale.mm`; CHANGED `ViewController+PageMenu.mm` (the "Set Scale..." item inserted first; S3 owns the file, 6.1; the presenter method of `ViewController+PageScale.mm` that the item calls is declared in S4's marker block `M1 pages B (S4)` of `ViewController_Internal.h`)
- NEW tests in existing areas: Geometry (transform), Formatting (presets, labels, form rules), Persistence (store, twin), Settings (undo)

**Touch adaptation.** Entry: "Set Scale..." in the page action menu (4.8), acting on the pressed page; the Windows bar combo is not ported, but its label rule (`scale_display_label`, group "bar 4") shows the page's current scale as the subtitle of the "Set Scale..." menu item and as the first footer line of the form. The form is a `UINavigationController` form sheet (inset grouped table like Edit Condition): style `UISegmentedControl` (header "Scale style:"; the first build had "Scale Style"), "Predefined scale" row (the first build had "Pre-defined scale") with an inline `UIPickerView` (as built: a pull-down `UIMenu` on that row, with the selected preset marked, same presets in the same order from Core `scale_presets`; a pick turns the Predefined radio on; a `UIPickerView` is not in the `check_objc` stubs and a `UIMenu` is system-anchored, 4.3), "Custom scale" row with two fields using `UIKeyboardTypeNumbersAndPunctuation` (a decimal pad offers only the locale separator, so '.' could not be typed in a comma locale; ',' stays refused with the Windows text) and a footer, "Apply to all pages" switch; Cancel + Save only (the form stays open when a save is refused); Windows alert texts in `UIAlertController`s ("Invalid Scale": "Select a predefined scale.", "Custom scale values cannot be empty.", "The first|second custom scale value "<text>" is not a valid number.", "Custom scale values must be positive."; "Save Failed": "Failed to save page scale."; and, since parity pass 2, "Save Failed": "The scale was not changed because the takeoffs and annotations on the page cannot be stored at that scale." for a scale that would store a takeoff-like coordinate as 0 at three decimals or a non-finite one, Windows d26893c). One confirmation only when "apply to all" affects more than one page. iPhone and iPad use the same controller (centred form sheet on iPad, no popover inside the form). After Save: update `ProjectData`; reload PlanView (`_loadCurrentPageIntoPlanViewResetZoom:NO`) only when the active 2D page is among the changed pages, regenerate meshes when a page selected in 3D changed, refresh quantities when the active or a selected page changed (Windows `ui_event_coordinator.py:3318-3359`, 4.11). Because the menu acts on the pressed page, `page_dialog_context_is_current` (`:5401-5409`) is ported as "same bid, unlocked, the pressed page still exists as the same page": its "same active page" clause is dropped and the two Windows tests that pin it are adapted.

**Dependencies.** Lock guard; number formatters (4.6); shares the page menu (4.8) with rename and delete; delete page must cover the same 17 position tables (5.6). M2: paste redo and the snap PDF-index cache key use the scale; M3: OST export reads these columns (stale `ScaleStyle` / `IsCustomScale` / `Quantity`, register SS-8, SS-9); M4: overlay rect (5.15) and annotation rotation slots (5.13) must be rescaled here from day one.

**Risk.** High for data (irreversible bulk rewrite of every coordinate of one or all pages, no undo): 95 Windows tests to port as oracles, one all-or-nothing transaction, whole-database rollback tests (all planned, none written). New iOS requirement: undo entries must capture the page scale or the history corrupts positions after a scale change. Number-format parity with Python (`%.3f`, round, `%.15g`, -0) must be checked on MSVC and clang. Legend XML is rewritten in place (the bytes outside the dX / dY values are kept), so the `xml.etree` normalisation of the Windows oracles is not reproduced (7 adapted oracles, see Tests-first plan). iOS stores memo columns as TEXT, so the rewrite binds text. PlanView caches the scale at `loadPage`.

**Size and order of work.** L (all figures are estimates: about 1,100 Core lines, ~110-130 tests, ~350 Objective-C lines). Order: (1) transform + formatter oracles red; (2) store tests red (incl. whole-database snapshot and late-table `block` rollback); (3) Core + store; (4) memory twin + agreement test; (5) undo capture + replay test; (6) form + menu item; (7) device pack rows.

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register SS-1 to SS-14; SS-6, SS-7, SS-11, SS-13 and SS-14 are in section 8): SS-1 `page_operations.py:67-78` - a NULL / 0 / negative / NaN / inf stored ScaleFactor makes every Set Scale on the page fail, no repair -> keep the refusal, nothing written (repair needs the owner: N-M1-22). SS-2 `page_operations.py:194-216`, `serialization.py:18-28` - no overflow guard, a huge factor writes `inf` text and iOS refuses to load such a takeoff (`ProjectStore.hpp:66`) -> refuse the whole change when the factor, any scaled coordinate is non-finite or a non-zero coordinate underflows to 0 (Windows d26893c refuses it too, `legend_position.py:7-17` `require_storable_scaled_values`: non-finite anywhere, and a non-zero takeoff-like coordinate that rounds to 0 at the stored 3 decimals; the 13 annotation tables and the overlay rectangle only get the non-finite check. iOS, parity pass 2: the 3-decimal rule for `BidTakeoffs`, `BidComments` and `BidTypGroupViews`, exact zero for the 13 annotation tables, the legend and the overlay rectangle, which is stricter than Windows and kept). SS-3 `set_scale_dialog.py:246-262` - nan / inf accepted -> refused in the form with `The first|second custom scale value "<text>" is not a valid number.` SS-4 `page_scale_transform.py:9-20` vs `overlay.py:7-19` vs `page_visualization_metadata_service.py:20-22`; `project_data_service.py:398-408` vs `page_operations.py:112-118` - three ratio definitions -> two Core rules, each pinned; the 3D rule `page_scale_ratio` stays (4.10). SS-5 `set_scale_dialog.py:149-150`, `:348-350` - fields filled with `{:g}` (6 digits): ticking only "apply to all" re-saves the truncated value and rescales by the truncation error (up to ~5e-6 relative; ~8e-9 for 12.0000001 -> 12) -> fill with full precision (Windows d26893c does too, `repr`, `set_scale_dialog.py:178-179`, `:416-418`; iOS, parity pass 2: the shortest text that reads back to the stored number, 1/3 -> 0.3333333333333333, where the first build used `%.15g`). SS-8 / SS-9 only `ScaleFactor1/2` written, `Quantity`, `GridOffsetX/Y`, totals, `BidAreaTranslations` not rescaled -> kept (parity), M3 export must not trust them. SS-10 `legend_position.py:6-26` - `xml.etree` + latin-1: a non-latin-1 character fails the whole change -> rewrite dX / dY in place, UTF-8; the text is not normalised, so the 7 legend oracles that expect the `xml.etree` byte form are adapted (Tests-first plan). Windows d26893c writes a non-latin-1 character as a character reference (`&#8364;`) instead of failing, but the bytes of a legend that iOS rewrote (raw UTF-8) still differ from those Windows writes (a known difference the M3 round trip must know). SS-12 `project_write_service.py:373-379` - a page of another bid would be rescaled -> refused.

**As built (M1).** Core: `PageScale.*` (presets, matching, labels), `PageScaleRules.*` (form rules and session), `PageScaleApply.*` (apply-all confirmation, footer, picker row, refresh plan after a save), `PageScaleCapture.*` (the undo lookup), `PageScaleTransform.*`, `PageScaleLegend.cpp` (the in-place legend rewrite behind a hand-written well-formedness scanner), `OverlayRect.*`, and the formatters in `NumberText.*`. Store `save_page_scales(db_path, bid_uid, page_uids, sf1, sf2, outcome*)` and the wrapper `save_page_scale` (`ProjectStore+PageScale.cpp`); bridge `+[ImportService savePageScales:inBid:scaleFactor1:scaleFactor2:locked:errorText:]`. UI: `Controllers/PageScaleViewController.{h,mm}` (not in a sub-folder as 5.4 writes it) and `ViewController+PageScale.mm`; the keyboard of the two custom fields is `UIKeyboardTypeNumbersAndPunctuation`, Save reads the live field text (a field is copied only when it is in a window), the keyboard scroll goes to the Top, one Set Scale form at a time, Cancel closes at once and ignores a late Save. Differences from the text above: (1) eight legend oracles are adapted, not seven (`test_page_scale_geometry.py:21` is the eighth); (2) the scanner differs from expat on four kinds of document that no OST writer produces (a DOCTYPE is refused, a damaged XML declaration is accepted, names with a colon are accepted, a default namespace is ignored); a fuzz test fails on any new kind of disagreement; (3) an unknown page at creation of an undo entry captures nothing (factor 1) and an unknown page at replay refuses the undo; (4) a page whose stored scale is NULL or 0 opens as 1 : 1 (the loader coalesces), and every Save on it is refused with the generic "Save Failed" text (N-M1-22; open point 9a.7); (5) the save is synchronous on the main thread; (6) the capture lookup is O(n x m) over the project takeoffs (a group move of 2000 takeoffs in a 50,000-takeoff project would hitch); both are judged on device check M1-17 with the largest bid.

**Tests-first plan (M1).** Port the 95 oracles in groups: transform 12, labels 5, store / content 32, legend 13 (the 13 of the 16 tests of `test_legend_position.py` that do not feed bytes / bytearray / latin-1 input, `:9`, `:22`, `:173`; 7 of them expect the `xml.etree` byte form `<X ... />` of an empty element (`:53`, `:71`, `:83`, `:92`, `:96`, `:119`, `:169`), which the in-place rewrite of SS-10 does not produce, so those 7 are ADAPTED: same inputs and same expected dX / dY values (the 3-decimal trim table, scaling, independent X and Y, attribute order, zero factor), the expected text being the input with only the values replaced; the other 6 port by intent: non-Legends root refused, malformed XML refused, comments and processing instructions kept, text and tail kept, non-finite refused, non-numeric refused), geometry (`test_page_scale_geometry.py`) 2, overlay calibration (`test_overlay_storage_calibration.py`) 2, memory twin 9 (7 ported 1:1, 2 adapted: see the iOS files above), service rules 4 (`test_project_write_service.py:781`, `:836`, `:873-904`, `:906`), bar 4, dialog session 6, undo 1, context 5 (total 95; 11 of them are adapted because of a listed deliberate difference, each saying so in its name or comment as the conventions of `Config/Tests/README.md` require, and no tolerance is relaxed: the 7 legend tests above, the 2 memory-twin tests and the 2 context tests that pin the "same active page" clause, see the iOS files and Touch adaptation). One more, not counted: `tests/integration/geometry/test_page_scale_projection.py:19-41` (a rescaled takeoff keeps its sheet position). Number-format oracles from Python (`%.3f` trimmed, `%.15g`, `{:g}`, -0) compared on MSVC and AppleClang. Data safety (new): whole-database snapshot equals the oracle after a refusal (every table not touched stays byte-identical); `TempDb::block` on a late table rolls back every earlier write; inf factor, underflow to 0, malformed rect, malformed legend, foreign-bid page, NULL stored scale, lock; memory == reload; undo replay after a scale change (the Core helper `rescale_entry_positions` driven by a stored page uid and captured pair with no takeoff in the project: the pinned `test_plan_view_action_handler.py:3849` scenario; the controller and delegate path itself is device-only, M1-17); Windows expected tolerances kept (0.0022 after 40 steps).

### 5.5 Rename page (S, risk low)

**What Windows does.** Rename Page (Edit menu, context menus) opens a modal dialog on the ACTIVE page (read-only 'Old Name', editable 'New Name', OK / Cancel; the labels read "Old name:" / "New name" since Windows d26893c) with 'Previous Page' / 'Next Page' buttons that step through the pages of the open bid in page-selector order and discard the typed draft. The only rule: strip the text (`str.strip()`), refuse empty with "Validation Error" / "Page name cannot be empty.", do nothing when equal to the stored name, else one statement `UPDATE [BidPages] SET [Name]=? WHERE [UID]=?` after an ownership check (positive uid, row exists, bid exists), in one transaction. The name is projected into memory in place and only the selector labels and the status-panel page text refresh (plus the Summary tab reload from memory when it is active); no plan view, no 3D, no undo. Failure: "Save Failed" / "Failed to rename page.". Duplicates, special characters and any length are accepted by the code (column VARCHAR(255)); everything refers to a page by UID.

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/rename_page_dialog.py:12-196` - dialog and `_on_ok` (`:136-148`: strip, empty refusal, unchanged accepted, else save)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/ui_event_coordinator.py:5714-5834`, `:5401-5409`, `:3318-3359`, `:4820-4847` - open guard, `_save_page_name`, `_on_page_metadata_changed` (labels only)
- `ost_visualizer/application/services/project_write_service.py:6341-6369`, `:381-415` - `save_page_name`: locked-bid guard, one mutation, in-place apply, reload fallback
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/page_operations.py:130-152` - SQL: `require_single_bid_scope_for_uids` then the UPDATE
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/database/bid_owned_identity.py:105-124`, `:144-152`, `:205-235` - uid and ownership refusals
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/mdb_writer.py:63-105`, `:276-302` - transaction, `_execute_update_values` (ignores the row count)
- `ost_visualizer/domain/services/project_data_service.py:420-447` - `apply_page_name`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/page_combo.py:19-29`, `:174-205`, `:280-291` - label format, refresh without selection signal
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/hierarchy_reader.py:211-241` - page order `Sequence, Name, UID` at load
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/database_creator.py:369-373` - `BidPages` (`[UID] COUNTER` at :370, `[Name] VARCHAR(255)` at :373); the 50-character `Name` at :356 belongs to `BidPageFolders` and is not touched

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_page_operations.py` (`PageOperationsRealContractTests`: `:1260` page 20 only, `:1269` missing Name column, `:1401`, `:1500` malformed uids 'abc', '0', '-5', ''); `tests/integration/mdb/test_ownership_and_atomicity.py:448` (orphan page); `tests/integration/mdb/test_project_roundtrip.py:32` (rename `:252-256`); `tests/domain/services/test_project_data_service.py:2436`, `:4047`; `tests/presentation/coordinators/test_ui_event_coordinator.py` (`Sp3a5hRenamePageDialogTests :31253` open-guard decision table, `:35841`); `tests/presentation/components/test_page_combo.py` (labels; first four already ported in iOS `Config/Tests/Persistence/test_page_navigation.cpp:429-457`; the Windows test `test_page_combo.py:1182` ('5 - S9 - Renamed', asserted at `:1196`) is not ported yet). 21 portable in part (13 store / domain / label + 8 rows of the open-guard table). Also `tests/presentation/dialogs/test_rename_page_dialog.py:163-197` (failure text `Save Failed` / `Failed to rename page.` at `:188-190`; controls re-enabled, draft kept; the rest of the file is focus handling and is not portable) and `tests/presentation/coordinators/test_ui_event_coordinator.py:14307-14358` (the same draft-kept behaviour on the queued path). NO Windows test pins blank, strip, unchanged, case-only change or duplicates: those are pinned from `rename_page_dialog.py:141-148` (estimate ~50 host tests: 14 rule, 8 predicate, 24 store, 5 in-memory).

- NEW Core: `Core/Domain/PageRules.hpp/.cpp` (`normalize_page_name`, whose 255-unit limit is counted by S0's `ost_domain::utf16_length`, `decide_page_rename`, texts, `can_rename_page`); `ProjectData::apply_page_name`
- NEW store: `Components/ProjectStore+PageRename.cpp` + `ProjectStore_PageRename.hpp` (`rename_page(db_path, bid_uid, page_uid, new_name, error*)`: one transaction, `parse_uid` x2, `bid_exists`, `row_belongs_to_bid(BidPages)`, lock, `normalize_page_name` re-checked (strip, blank, over 255 UTF-16 units by `ost_domain::utf16_length`, NUL), `UPDATE ... WHERE UID=? AND BidUID=?`, then `SELECT changes()` must be exactly 1 (no row-count accessor exists; no new API, no edit of `OstDatabase.*`), else rollback; the zero-row path is tested with a `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger that does `RAISE(IGNORE)`, because `TempDb::block` uses `RAISE(ABORT)`)
