- BUILD (only after N-M3-01): `project.yml`, `.github/workflows/build.yml`, `BUILD.md`, `Config/Project.plist`, `Config/Tests/CMakeLists.txt` (QPDF build / cache / link / fetch; `Core/Export/pdf/pdf_writer.cpp` goes into `OST_CORE_EXCLUDE` and is added back only with `OST_TEST_QPDF`, with a stub like `Config/Tests/Support/BooleanOpsStub.cpp` so that `pdf_export.cpp` links without QPDF, because the `Core/*.cpp` glob at `Config/Tests/CMakeLists.txt:45-47` would otherwise compile it without QPDF headers (the exclusion list is `:48-51`, the stub branch `:54-59`); the app build compiles it through the `Core` glob of `project.yml`, so header / library paths are set there first; a workflow like `build-manifold-ios.yml` builds `libqpdf.a`), `Libs/qpdf` (headers + `libqpdf.a`) or CI-built and cached
- NEW tests: `Config/Tests/Pdf/test_*.cpp` (+ generator test support, `OST_TEST_QPDF` option), `Config/Tests/Settings/test_pdf_export_options.cpp`

**Touch adaptation.** Entry in the existing export action sheet (iPad popover anchor); the options sheet is a modal form sheet (iPad) or page sheet (iPhone) and needs no popover anchor (as in 5.10); only a popover (for example the colour picker if it is shown as one) or an action sheet opened from it needs `sourceView` / `sourceRect` (4.3). Options as a form sheet (iPad) or page sheet (iPhone): captions switch + 9 rows in fixed order, PDF elevation callouts switch + 4 rows + colour (`UIColorPickerViewController`), Windows defaults (captions off, callouts off, 4 content flags on, #ff0000). Colour and opacity use the 2D view's own inputs (the `renderConfig` display mode and grayscale through `display_opacity_2d`), which stand in for Windows `display_mode_2d` and `grayscale_enabled` (`export_handler.py:256-257`, `pdf_exporter.py:155-157`; pinned by `test_export_handler.py:653`). Page selection as in the other exports plus a one-tap "current page"; PROJECT order (Sequence, Name, UID), not a UID-sorted `std::set` (a deliberate difference: Windows exports in tick order, N-M3-16). Snapshot `ProjectData`, build on a background queue with progress (index, total, page name) and a Cancel (iOS addition), background task, ONE QPDF object per source file for memory; write to the temp folder, never next to the source PDFs, then the share sheet; name by the Windows PDF rule (`export_handler.py:188-204`: one page `<bid> - <page>`, `.pdf` appended unless the page name already ends in `.pdf` in any case, a blank page name is `Page`; several pages `<bid> - <N> Pages.pdf`; tests `test_export_handler.py:489-538`) through `sanitize_filename`, capped at 255 UTF-8 bytes (the APFS limit and the unit of C-8) cut at a code-point boundary, a new function next to `export_filename` (that function is the OBJ / DXF rule and caps by code points); name the page with unreadable geometry in the error. **Windows texts kept verbatim:** `No Valid Pages` / `No valid pages selected for export.` (`export_handler.py:192-194`); `Successfully exported page to {filename}` / `Successfully exported {N} pages to {filename}` (`:284-288`).

**Dependencies.** QPDF 12.3.2 as an iOS arm64 static library; a CI SPIKE (estimate: 1-2 days) gates the feature (C++ standard, size, Nyxian link, licence notice, native crypto: N-M3-01); zlib already linked; M1 Set Scale supplies valid `scale_factor1/2` (an unscaled page makes the whole export fail once a takeoff exists); M4 supplies the annotation model, overlay drawing and image modes for the extension; shares plumbing with 5.10 / 5.11. PDFKit and CoreGraphics cannot reproduce the markup contract.

**Risk.** HIGH until the QPDF-on-iOS spike passes, then medium: nothing builds QPDF for iOS today (size, C++ standard, CI time, Nyxian, licence); Bluebeam and Preview compatibility and runtime behaviour can only be verified on a device; the writer's clipping depends on QPDF 12.3.2 internals (`TrimBox`, `getFormXObjectForPage`, `placeFormXObject`); memory on large CAD sheets. The logic port itself is low risk (text and number oracles; iOS already has the geometry core).

**Size and order of work.** L. Order: (0) the CI spike (owner decision N-M3-01 first); (1) number / caption / callout / polygon generators with oracle tests, QPDF-free; (2) page-info and canonical-page matrix (64 cases) as pure maths; (3) QPDF writer on the host (`OST_TEST_QPDF`); (4) the iOS static library + writer in the app; (5) options sheet, export item, share, progress / cancel; (6) device pack rows (Bluebeam and Preview open the file; a rotated, cropped, UserUnit sheet).

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register P-1 to P-14): P-1 `bluebeam_annotation.cpp:246-260`, `:961-970`, `:1461-1470` - `/RC` gets XML-escaped text but no PDF escaping, an unbalanced `)` or a backslash fails the whole export (no test) -> escape `( ) \` in `/RC` too. P-2 `:202`, `:239`, `:649-654`, `:1452-1470` - non-ASCII text is written as raw UTF-8 into `/Contents`, `/Label`, `/RC`, `/T` and appearance streams (mojibake in Bluebeam) -> UTF-16BE (BOM) when any byte >= 0x80, WinAnsi with `?` in appearance streams. P-3 numbers streamed with the default ostream format (6 significant digits, exponent form below 1e-4 = invalid PDF number syntax) -> keep the 6-digit text, plain decimals everywhere. P-4 `bluebeam_annotation.cpp:179-184`, `:279`, `:288` - the `/Measure` scale ignores `scale_factor2` (right only when sf2 == 12) -> byte output unchanged for sf2 == 12 (pinned), for other pairs feet per point = `sf2/(12*sf1*72)` with a matching `/R` text. P-5 `pdf_exporter.py:559-563` - an out-of-range page index is refused (the plan view clamps) -> keep the refusal and name the page. P-6 `export_handler.py:182-190`, `:284-287` - pages with a non-positive stored size are silently dropped -> list the skipped pages. P-7 `pdf_writer.cpp:275-286`, `:313` - `/AP` only for `area_sf > 0` -> always write the appearance stream, `/Measure` only for positive area. P-8 `pdf_writer.cpp:1073-1104` - one QPDF per exported page, all alive until the write -> one per distinct source path. P-9 `pdf_writer.cpp:1117-1119` - partial file on failure -> temp name, move on success. P-12 caption lengths always mm, depth inches (mirrors `/Measure /D`, pinned) -> kept, noted. P-13 the PDF uses the true footprint without view minimums -> kept. P-14 caption text relies on Python `round()` (ties to even) and exact `%.2f` -> `std::nearbyint`, exact `%.2f`, own thousands separator, no locale.

**Texts (owner decision EXP-21, job 11).** All alert, progress and note texts of this feature are the Windows sentences of section 4.15; the writer returns a typed result and the Core text module builds the sentence; no raw error ever reaches the alert.

**MILESTONE M4 - annotations, views, 3D**

### 5.13 Annotations and hotlinks (named views as the hotlink target) on the 2D plan (L, risk HIGH)

**What Windows does.** 13 annotation families in 13 tables (12 drawable; callouts are only stored); two of them are special kinds: NAMED VIEWS (a green rectangle with a name) and HOTLINKS (a marker that jumps to a named view; there is NO URL and NO page-only link). Positions keep every index (rotation slot rules), colours are BGR integers. Writes are one atomic typed mutation per batch behind the lock guard; a named view cannot be deleted while a hotlink targets it (the delete plan includes the hotlinks). A hotlink click resolves the target page and opens it according to the option 'Open Hot Links in' (annotation window = the Windows default, view window, main window: `config.py:23-28`, `open_annotation_view_use_case.py:46-58`). iOS implements only the 'main' behaviour (navigate the main plan, then zoom to the named view, margin 0.1: `ui_event_coordinator.py:1285-1295`, `named_view_focus.py:3-13`); the two detached-window targets are out of scope (section 10). Windows also draws condition labels (dimension text for areas, name text) from the takeoff font columns; they are not on your list, so they are NOT planned (N-M4-23).

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/domain/entities/annotation.py:5-254` (13 type names, rotation-slot rule `:20-27`, bbox, BGR to hex), `annotation_style.py:5-97` (defaults red / width 4 / Arial 12; limits width 1..16, font 1..144, align 0..2), `named_view.py:21-87` (9-value stored corner order, 8-value edit order), `hotlink.py:6-33`
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/annotation_reader.py:27-480` - one query per table, defaults, rows without a usable position dropped, layer default to the annotation layer
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/annotation_operations.py:67-677` - save positions / text / styles (`:67-80`), insert (validate bid, page, layer, target before uid allocation), delete (named view needs every hotlink)
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/serialization.py:5-81` - position text with every index kept, text columns, Latin-1 annotation text
- `ost_visualizer/application/services/annotation_write_service.py:29-224` - locked-bid guard (`:118` vs `:171`), one atomic mutation per batch
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/services/annotation_write_coordinator.py:101-331`, `annotation_history.py:11-238` - write / project / publish, undo dependency of a hotlink on its named view
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/utils/annotation_defaults.py:25-252`, `annotation_delete.py:5-92`, `annotation_paste.py:4-58`, `named_view_validation.py:5-31`, `named_view_focus.py:3-13`
- `ost_visualizer/application/use_cases/annotation_view/open_annotation_view_use_case.py:27-68`, `ui_event_coordinator.py:1285-1395` - hotlink click
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/pdf/renderers/annotation_renderer.py:30-512`, `annotation_item_renderer.py:57-543` - one geometry description per family (highlight quads, cloud scallops, dimension ticks and label), font factor 0.75, hotlink radius 15 pt
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/pdf/renderers/takeoff_renderer.py:136-359`, `infrastructure/mdb/components/takeoff_operations.py:46-59`, `:297-332` - condition labels, font columns (reference for N-M4-23 only; not planned)

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_annotation_operations.py` (96: rollback `:470`, `:2126`; orphan page / layer / target refused before uid allocation `:207`, `:1292`, `:1327`, `:1358`; named view delete needs every hotlink `:1831`, `:2553`; numeric 0 target stored NULL `:1162`; unsupported type `:425`, `:1894`; cross-bid `:351`, `:3238`); `test_annotation_reader.py` (29); `tests/presentation/visualization/pdf/renderers/test_annotation_renderer.py` (95: `format_dimension_distance` 255.0 -> `21' - 3"`, highlight quads, cloud scallops `:654-800`); `tests/presentation/services/test_annotation_write_coordinator.py` (60) and `test_annotation_history.py` (33); `tests/presentation/utils/test_annotation_defaults.py`, `test_annotation_delete.py`, `test_annotation_paste.py`; `tests/integration/geometry/test_annotation_scale_contracts.py`, `tests/integration/placement/test_annotation_boundaries.py`; `tests/integration/annotations/test_layer_ownership.py`. About 215 of the 363 test functions in the files listed port (the Qt-only ones do not; estimate 260 host cases; an estimate, not a measured count); Qt plan-view and dialog tests do not port and are the behaviour reference.

**iOS today / iOS files touched.** The 13 tables exist (`Core/Persistence/OstSchema.cpp:18-70`, `:88-113`, `:220-259`, `:325-332`, `:519-532`), the importer writes them, integrity and delete cascades cover them; `PositionParser.hpp` (`parse_position`, `encode_position`), `DimensionFormat.hpp`, `placement_snap.hpp`, `geom_utils.hpp`, `coord_transform.hpp`, `Layer.hpp`, `UndoHistory.hpp` (generic; `UndoRedoController` is takeoff-only), PlanView overlay drawing, gestures, handles. NOT present: model, reader, renderer, tools, text drawing, condition labels, annotation layer uid, rect zoom. New / changed:
- NEW Core: `Core/Domain/Annotation.hpp/.cpp` (`annotation_rotation_index` is the M1 function in `PageScaleTransform.cpp`, 5.4: `rescale_position_text` keeps the rotation slot of the 13 annotation tables from day one; `Annotation.hpp` calls it, no second copy), `NamedView.hpp/.cpp`, `Hotlink.hpp`, `AnnotationPlan.hpp/.cpp` (delete order, copy filter, hotlink dependency); `Core/Geometry/annotation_geometry.hpp/.cpp`, `annotation_edit.hpp/.cpp`
- NEW store: `Components/ProjectStore+Annotations.cpp` + `ProjectStore_Annotations.hpp` (`load_annotations`, `insert_annotations`, `delete_annotations`, `save_annotation_positions` / `text_properties` / `styles`), all transactional behind the lock guard (4.1) and `next_uid_within_access_limit` (4.2: `insert_annotations` allocates UIDs in 13 tables, `annotation_operations.py:354-364`); NEW bridge `Services/ImportService+Annotations.h/.mm`
- NEW UI: `PlanView/Components/AnnotationOverlayView.h/.mm`, `PlanView+Annotations.mm`, `Controllers/AnnotationTextViewController.*`, `NamedViewPickerViewController.*`; CHANGED `PlanView.*`, `PlanView+Placement.mm`, `TakeoffOverlayView.mm`, `ViewController.mm`, `UndoRedoController.*` (typed entries), `ViewController+PageSelector.mm`, `FloatingModeButton.mm`, `Core/Domain/EditMode.hpp`
- NEW tests: `Config/Tests/Annotations/` (+ oracle headers)

**Touch adaptation.** iPhone: a tool picker (sheet) arms a PlaceAnnotation mode via the floating mode button; one-finger drag places line / arrow / dimension / rect / oval / highlight / text box / named view with the Windows minimum size (snap increment, else 1e-6); polygon / cloud by taps with a 12 pt close or a Done chip (self-intersection refused); ink by stroke; hotlink by tap. A tap on a hotlink navigates; a long-press selects it for Delete / Properties (Windows navigates on click and selects by rubber band, which touch lacks). Handles stay 4 pt visually with a 22 pt hit radius. Text entry in a MODAL multi-line sheet with a style row (not inline: keyboard avoidance on a zoomed canvas is the device risk); named-view name by an alert with a uniqueness check; the named-view picker is a searchable table. No keyboard nudge, no rubber band; hotlinks open in the main plan (detached windows are out of scope). iPad: tool picker as an anchored popover, properties popover from the selection, named-view picker as a form sheet. **Windows texts kept verbatim:** `Named View` / `Named view should have unique name` (`named_view_validation.py:7`, `:31`); `Database Write` / `The annotations could not be saved. Refresh the Bid and verify its Page and Layer settings before trying again. See the log for details.` (`plan_view_action_handler.py:3484-3489`); the title `Hot Link History` (`:1604`; the text is the dependency error's own message).

**Dependencies.** M1 MUST already handle annotation data: Set Scale rescales every annotation / named-view / hotlink position with the rotation slot kept (`page_operations.py:154-217`), Delete Page cascades incl. hotlinks targeting its named views, layer delete checks the annotation tables; the lock guard (4.1); M2 snap resolver (helpful, not required) and the M2 clipboard (takeoffs only in M2; 5.13 adds annotation copy / paste to it, `annotation_paste.py:4-58`, `paste_ref_remap_dto.py:13-35`); M3 OST export writes the tables with the Windows value forms and the Latin-1 rule; the PDF extension (N-M3-17) reuses the geometry. UIKit, CoreGraphics (multiply blend for highlights), CoreText / `NSAttributedString`; no new library.

**Risk.** HIGH: 13 families, 7 gesture families, text editing, typed undo, two new overlay layers; iOS and Windows edit the same files, so value forms must match exactly (every position index, rotation slots, named-view 9-value corner order, the named-view insert writes Origin 0 (`annotation_operations.py:577`), Latin-1 text, NULL vs 0 hotlink target, width 0 reads back as 2.0, BGR colour ints); text rendering parity and touch behaviour cannot be proven on the Windows host; M1 shipping Set Scale / Delete Page / layer delete without annotation-aware cascades would corrupt data.

**Size and order of work.** L (several sessions). Order: (1) Core model + geometry port with oracles; (2) read-only drawing of all 12 families + tap-to-navigate; (3) store with lock guard; (4) editing wave 1: text, named view, hotlink, rect, oval; (5) wave 2: line, arrow, dimension; (6) wave 3: polygon, cloud, ink, highlight; (6a) annotation copy / paste on the M2 clipboard (takeoff link remap: an unmapped takeoff link is dropped, A-11; a pasted hotlink KEEPS its named-view target when that view belongs to the destination bid, because named views are not copied (`plan_view_action_handler.py:4553`) and `PasteRefRemap` leaves an unmapped `BidPageViewUID` unchanged (`paste_ref_remap_dto.py:27-35`); pasted into another bid, where the Windows insert would fail the whole paste (`annotation_operations.py:351-353`), the hotlink target becomes NULL: a deliberate safe difference, register row A-16); (7) typed undo, device pack rows; (8) PDF extension (N-M3-17): the 9 annotation generators and the annotation collection added to the M3 PDF.

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register A-1 to A-16): A-1 `ost_visualizer/domain/utils/position.py:11-17`, `serialization.py:18-43`, `annotation_reader.py:49-50` - NaN / inf positions are accepted, written and drawn -> refuse non-finite on write, skip such a row on read and leave it untouched. A-2 `annotation_operations.py:75-78` vs `:330-334`, `:633-635` - position save skips an unknown type and returns True, insert and delete raise -> refuse unknown types everywhere. A-3 `annotation_operations.py:246-255`, `:279-299` - domain clamps are not applied by persistence (width 0 reads back as 2.0) -> clamp with the domain normalisers on write and read. A-4 `plan_view/view.py:1739-1747` vs `plan_view_action_handler.py:3111` - blank text refused on create but an empty edit of an existing text is saved (invisible) -> an empty edit is a cancel. A-6 `named_view_validation.py:13-27` - uniqueness only in the dialog -> enforce (trim, case-fold) in Core on create / rename, never rewrite existing duplicates. A-8 `annotation_renderer.py:186-196` - the dimension label is always imperial (even on metric bids) -> kept (display only, nothing is stored, so nothing can be corrupted; the owner may choose a metric label), flagged (N-M4-07). A-9 no ORDER BY: draw order and picker order depend on row order -> sort by uid per family, picker by case-folded name then uid. A-14 `annotation_write_service.py:118` vs `:171` - the lock check passes the bid uid only for insert -> always the bid of the write. A-15 iOS schema trap, not a Windows defect: `BidHotLinks.BidPageViewUID DEFAULT 0` (`OstSchema.cpp:256`) vs NULL for "no target" -> write NULL explicitly. A-16 `paste_ref_remap_dto.py:27-35`, `annotation_operations.py:328-353` - named views are never copied (`plan_view_action_handler.py:4553`), so a pasted hotlink keeps its old target; the Windows insert accepts that inside the same bid and fails the WHOLE paste in another bid (`BidNamedViews` is checked against the destination bid) -> same bid keeps the target, a paste into another bid writes a NULL target and goes on (a hotlink with no target shows a one-line toast, N-M4-02).

### 5.14 Image modes: Invert, Bitonal, Adjust (orientation), page show mode (M, risk medium)

**What Windows does.** There is NO brightness / contrast / threshold. "Adjust Images" = rotation (0 / 90 / 180 / 270) + flip X / Y + Invert + Bitonal + apply-to-all, stored in `BidPages.Rotation / FlipX / FlipY / Invert / Bitonal`; Show (0 original, 1 overlay, 2 both) is separate. "Bitonal" is a paper cap (each channel `min(c, 220)`, Qt Darken), Invert is `255 - c` with alpha kept, order bitonal then invert (paper 255 / 220 / 0 / 35). Both-mode = the original tinted red (255,80,80) and the overlay blue (80,80,255) by a portable C++ kernel (alpha = `clamp(((235-gray)*255+117)/235, 1, 255)` below gray 235). Effects run after every cache on worker threads; rotation / flip are a SCENE transform, not a raster op.

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/utils/image_effects.py:11-53` (`tint_image`, `invert_image`, `bitonal_image`, `page_effect_paper_color`, effect order), `image_bands.py:5-101` (banding 262144 px)
- `cpp_extensions/src/image/image_tint.cpp:5-44` (kernel), `cpp_extensions/src/module_image.cpp:18-53` (binding, 'Buffer too small')
- `cpp_extensions/src/pdf/pdf_renderer.cpp:1164-1179` - raster filled opaque white (pages are always opaque)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/pdf/services/pdf_rendering_service.py:147-272`, `composite_renderer.py:57-192`, `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/pdf/page_cache.py:17-33`, `:395-444` - effects after the cache, Both composite, cache budgets (not iOS-safe)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/plan_view/components/zoom_handler.py:339-348` - page transform: rotate(-rotation) (counter-clockwise), flips; `plan_view/view.py:2531-2576`, `:4289-4393`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/utils/image_show_mode.py:1-33` - modes, `resolve_toggled_mode` (cannot switch both off)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/ui_event_coordinator.py:5231-5281`, `:5283-5461`, `:6062-6093`, `:6189-6246` - rotate / flip commands, Adjust Images dialog and apply-to-all, show-mode toggles

**Touch adaptation.** ONE "Image" button in the 2D toolbar (the merged control of 4.9): bottom sheet on iPhone, anchored popover on iPad. Orientation: segmented 0 / 90 / 180 / 270 plus flip switches and two quick turn buttons labelled by the VISIBLE direction (Windows "Rotate Image Left" actually turns the page clockwise). Invert and Bitonal switches save immediately (one transaction each; a failure leaves memory and DB unchanged and shows an alert). "Apply to all pages" is a separate confirmed button that writes ONLY the changed fields. Show mode = a 3-segment control Original | Overlay | Both, segments disabled when a source is missing (after 5.15 exists). Live view: CoreGraphics blend modes in `PDFCanvasView drawRect` after an opaque white fill (Darken 220 for Bitonal, Difference white for Invert) = `255 - min(c, 220)` exactly, no bitmap cache; the canvas colour follows the paper colour even with the Image layer hidden. Both mode and raster pages need a bitmap path with the shared budget (N-M4-13); keep zoom on change; 2D only (the 3D page plane is out of scope).

**Dependencies.** M1 page-write guards (bid scope, chunked UPDATE, one transaction: reuse, do not duplicate); 5.15 (Overlay and Both are meaningless until overlay drawing exists: build the show-mode UI and tint path last); M3: export keeps Invert / Bitonal / Show / RasterDrawMethod, PDF export must NOT apply the effects (Windows does not); frameworks: CoreGraphics (linked), Accelerate optional; Core C++17 only, no platform headers.

**Risk.** Medium. Low: persistence, show-mode resolver, rotation arithmetic, channel table, tint kernel (deterministic, host-testable). Medium: blend-mode drawing is device-only (CGPDF vs PDFium anti-aliasing; the destination must be opaque white first). Medium-high: Both mode needs a bitmap path, iPhone memory limits, a zoom-time re-render that iOS lacks, and overlay drawing that does not exist yet.

**Size and order of work.** Medium (small when limited to rotate, flip, invert, bitonal; large with Both mode). Order: (1) channel table / paper colour / rotation / show-mode tests red -> green; (2) load Invert / Bitonal + store; (3) Image sheet with rotate, flip, Invert, Bitonal (blend modes); (4) apply-to-all; (5) show mode + Both after 5.15; (6) device pack rows.

M-2 `ui_event_coordinator.py:5231-5235`, `zoom_handler.py:342-343` - "Rotate Image Left" = delta -90 = 270 = clockwise -> stored arithmetic kept, buttons labelled by visible direction. M-3 `ui_event_coordinator.py:5420-5461` - Apply to all writes all five fields to every page (overwrites other pages' rotation / flips) -> only the fields the user changed, with a confirmation naming the page count (N-M4-12). M-4 `page_operations.py:257-276`, `page_load_strategy_service.py:33-35`, `:52-57` - Show is unvalidated; a Show 1 naming a missing overlay (or Show 0 with no original) draws a blank page -> `effective_show_mode` always shows an existing source without rewriting the stored value, the store refuses values outside 0-2. M-6 `image_bands.py:74-81` - Invert uses `invertPixels` on any format -> straight-alpha BGRA, colour channels only. M-7 `image_effects.py:12` - the gray conversion is Qt Grayscale8 and no test uses a coloured pixel -> `qgray = (11r+16g+5b)/32` only after one read-only Windows oracle run. M-9 `page_cache.py:17-33` - budgets not iOS-safe -> not ported. M-10 `pdf_exporter.py:272-301` - PDF export ignores Invert / Bitonal -> kept.

### 5.15 Overlay image drawing: select, remove, show, move (L, risk medium)

**What Windows does.** The user picks a pdf / tif / tiff overlay per page (the rect resets to the whole page), removes it, toggles Show Original / Show Overlay (`BidPages.Show` 0 / 1 / 2) and MOVES it (rect x, y only). There is NO resize UI and NO rotate / deskew UI (`OverlayResized` is never written true; rotation and deskew are only read and drawn). Both-mode = base tinted (255,80,80) + overlay tinted (80,80,255), paper threshold 235, drawn translate, rotate (overlay + deskew, radians), non-uniform scale into `OverlayRect` (units = `sf2/sf1` per sheet inch, text "x,y,w,h", 6 decimals, "*" = empty).

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/domain/entities/overlay.py:4-19` (96 DPI, `overlay_units_per_sheet_inch`), `page.py:28-232` (overlay fields, effective size swapped only at 90 / 270, rect units <-> canvas, denominator = STORED page size)
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/overlay_rect.py:6-106` - parse / serialize (6 decimals), `*` empty marker, path identity, `full_page_overlay_rect`, `replacement_overlay_storage_values` (`:74`: Show 0 on remove, 1 when no original)
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/bid_data_reader.py:729-769` - loader (invalid rect logged and replaced by empty), Show raw int
- `ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/page_operations.py:57-128` (rect rescale on Set Scale), `:257-276`, `:278-376` - overlay image / rect / show writes (same-path no-op, single-bid guard)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/utils/image_show_mode.py:1-33`; `application/services/page_load_strategy_service.py:29-100`
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/pdf/services/composite_renderer.py:57-307`, `:309-720` - composite and overlay-only canvas, draw transform

About 85 port to Core host tests (estimate 110) + ~25 composite tests at transform / tint level; the rest (caches, cancellation, frames) and the ~55 Qt move tests have no iOS counterpart.

**Touch adaptation.** Entry: the overlay actions are sections of the ONE "Image" menu (4.9): Select, Remove, Show Overlay, Show Original, Move (popover on iPad, sheet on iPhone, every `UIAlertController` sheet with an anchor). Move mode: one-finger drag of the overlay or a 44 pt handle, two fingers still pan / pinch, repeated drags allowed, EXPLICIT Done and Cancel (no commit by tapping outside: Windows commits on any click outside the handle), no resize, no snapping, no undo. Save synchronously first; on a refused write restore the rect and show "The overlay position could not be saved." Picker: `UIDocumentPicker` pdf + tiff, `asCopy:YES`, copy into the folder of the page's own image (`Documents/OST/<package name>/`; a bid with no staged folder uses `Documents/OST/<bid name>/`) with the OSP non-conflicting name rule, bid / page identity re-checked on return. After Select on a page whose mode is Original, keep the mode (Windows) and show the hint "Turn on Show Overlay Image". Draw with CoreGraphics (a PDF overlay as vector in overlay-only; show-both rasterised per tile with the Windows tint); never allocate a full 3x RGBA sheet (~235 MB for 42x30 in). **Windows text kept verbatim:** `Overlay Selection Cancelled` / `The selected page changed while the file dialog was open. Please choose the overlay again.` (`ui_event_coordinator.py:6041-6046`), shown when the bid or page identity check fails on return from the picker.

**Dependencies.** M1 Set Scale MUST rescale `OverlayRect` and the offsets (`page_operations.py:57-128`) or an iOS scale change corrupts Windows pages with overlays; M1 page-write guards; M3: OST export keeps `OverlayRect` / `Show` / `OverlayResized` as stored, OSP export packs the overlay files (pdf / tif / tiff only, by name), PDF export draws overlays (extension N-M3-17); 5.14 (shared effects pass and tint). Out of scope but touching: Cover Sheet overlay setter, MCP read service, detached windows, 3D page plane.

**Risk.** Medium: show-both needs a per-pixel tint / blend that must be tiled on a device (no iOS tiling or raster decode exists); rotated / flipped pages are inconsistent between Windows modes; the first `BidPages` writes can corrupt a Windows project (mitigated by ported refusal tests and M1 landing the write path first); a touch drag must not move the page or commit by accident. The logic itself is small and covered by ~85 portable tests.

**Size and order of work.** L (estimate: ~40% Core + persistence, ~35% rendering, ~25% UI). Order: (1) pure Core + ported tests; (2) persistence writes; (3) overlay-only drawing + show toggles; (4) select / remove (picker, staging); (5) tint + show-both (tiled, `image_tint` from 5.14); (6) touch move mode; (7) device pack rows; (8) PDF extension (N-M3-17): the overlay / raster backgrounds added to the M3 PDF. Before step 6 the owner supplies the Windows result for a rotated page (N-M4-15).

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register V-1 to V-17): V-1 no resize exists although docs say so (`overlay_rect.py:99`, `bid_data_reader.py:760`) -> no handle; stored `OverlayResized` kept as loaded. V-2 `page_operations.py:286-306`, `overlay_rect.py:51-71` vs `test_page.py:437-445` - the whole-page rect uses the UNROTATED width / height but a rect lives in the rotated page -> full-page rect from the displayed size; flagged. V-3 `view.py:5096`, `:5197-5202`; `pdf_exporter.py:375-380`; `native_page_plane.py:93-101` - overlay-only 2D rotates the overlay by `page.rotation` and ignores flips, composite / PDF / 3D use rotation 0 (needs a Windows run to confirm) -> one rule: rect in the displayed canvas, overlay not rotated or flipped (N-M4-15). V-5 `page_load_strategy_service.py:34-35`, `:55` (pinned `test_page_load_strategy_service.py:138-152`) vs `overlay_context_menu.py:25-31` - Show 1 without an overlay shows a blank page while the menu says Original; Show 2 without an overlay draws the original -> a page without overlay is Original whatever Show says. V-7 `bid_data_reader.py:730-738`, `page_operations.py:294-298` - an overlay with an empty / invalid rect is invisible, unmovable and re-selecting the same file writes nothing -> regenerate the full-page rect. V-8 `page_operations.py:432-477` - rotate / flip of the image does not touch the rect -> kept, recorded, not silently fixed. V-11 `page.py:74-83`, `overlay_rect.py:67-68` - a page with stored Width / Height 0 can never show an overlay -> kept, with a visible reason (N-M4-17). V-13 the picker's "All Files" admits types OSP cannot carry -> pdf and tiff only. V-14 non-uniform stretch of a different-aspect overlay (pinned) -> kept.

### 5.16 3D section cuts (L, risk medium; the cap step high)

**What Windows does.** Two independent axis-aligned cuts, each a slider 0..1000 where 1000 is OFF. The state is a fraction of the CURRENT mesh bounds. East-west (native x) removes x > cut, north-south (native y) removes y > cut. The renderer discards the removed side in the opaque, OIT, pick and flat-colour passes, so picking never sees it. Cut faces are filled with CAPS: the mesh is sliced, loops chained, nested by even / odd depth and triangulated with earcut; caps sit 0.01 units inside, colour x 0.86, are pickable and outlined only with the edge overlay or when selected. The label is the distance from the minimum edge in feet ("off" or `{:,.1f}'`). Nothing is persisted and no exporter uses the cut.

**Windows modules.**
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/mesh_view.py:47-50`, `:98-99`, `:199-201`, `:976-1018` - cut ids, 12 units per foot, fractions init 1.0, `set_section_cut` (clamp, ValueError on unknown cut `:979-986`), offset feet, extent, re-applied on scene change
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/section_cut_controls.py:15-33`, `:54-69`, `:113-131` - sliders, `format_section_cut_value`
- `cpp_extensions/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:603-606`, `:615`, `:636-651`, `:728-733`, `:745-750`, `:1019-1020`, `:1274-1277`, `:1361-1372` - the removal rule in four fragment shaders with `discard`, uniform packing, `set_section_cut`
- `cpp_extensions/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:776-984`, `:1296-1360`, `:1941-1951`, `:2000-2006`, `:2031-2039`, `:2239`, `:2280`, `:2365`, `:2394-2400` - cap builder (slice, quantised chaining, nesting, earcut), `ensure_caps` (0.01 inset), caps in opaque / OIT / pick / selection passes, cap outlines
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/renderers/threejs/templates/viewer.html:2160-2243`, `:3020-3299` - the HTML-export twin (best geometry oracle)
- `AGENTS.md:446-461` (Windows repo; the written contract)

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/presentation/components/test_section_cut_controls.py` (`:51` cuts start off and map to native axes; `:69` right end = off, clamp -3 -> 0, 7 -> 1; `:80` a new scene re-applies the cut; `:96`; `:116`-`:149`: bounds x -120..0, y 78..2418: NS 0.25 -> position 663 and `48.8'`, EW 0.5 -> -60 and `5.0'`, `format(1234.56)` = `1,234.6'`); `tests/integration/native/test_renderer_section_cuts.py` (4; native GL, skipped offscreen); `tests/presentation/visualization/renderers/threejs/test_threejs_renderer.py` (`:1468` box slice areas 8 and 12, `:1495` box-in-box gives 1 hole, `:1520`) = the JS oracles for the cap builder. 11 directly portable (estimate 25-35 host cases with data-safety cases); the Windows native tests need GL and map to Core predicates and geometry.

**iOS today / iOS files touched.** Nothing of the feature exists (`PORTING_MAP.md:159`, was :152 before the M1 rows were added above it). `Core/Domain/SceneRendering.hpp:65-103` "clip planes" are camera near / far planes only. The mesh frame equals the HTML-export frame ((X,Y,Z) -> (X,Z,-Y), `TakeoffMeshBuilder.hpp:8-9`, `CameraFraming.hpp:13-15`); `Rendering/Renderer.mm:457-477` (mesh-only scene bounds, every frame), all fragment shaders already receive `v_worldPos` (`Rendering/Shaders/src/vs_mesh.sc:9`); pass structure `Renderer.mm:552-833`; `Core/Geometry/earcut.hpp` + `earcut_impl.cpp` under `strict_fp.hpp`; `Core/Domain/NumberText.hpp:14` (`round_half_even`, `format_double`) and the file-local `group_thousands` of `QuantityDisplay.cpp:13-24` (move it into NumberText in step 2; add a grouped `%.1f` form and host-test the pinned 48.75 -> `48.8'` (`test_section_cut_controls.py:116-149`) and one tie that tells half-even from half away from zero, 48.25 -> `48.2'` (the JS viewer's `toLocaleString`, `viewer.html:2222-2225`, would print `48.3'`; reasoned, not run)). On iOS the rule is EW removes x > pos and NS removes z < -pos, NS extent [-max_z, -min_z]. New / changed:
- NEW Core: `Core/Domain/SectionCut.hpp/.cpp` (state, extent, plane, feet, label, uniform, predicate), `Core/Geometry/section_cap.hpp/.cpp` (slice, chain, nest, earcut; port of `renderer.cpp:818-984`)
- CHANGED `Rendering/Shaders/src/fs_mesh.sc`, `fs_oit_accum.sc`, `fs_oit_revealage.sc`, `fs_meshid.sc` (+ regenerated `Rendering/Shaders/generated/fs_*.h`), `Rendering/Renderer.mm/.h` (uniform, per-pass use, cap buffers beside `_uploadMeshesToGpu :366-454`)
- NEW `Controllers/ViewController/Components/SectionCutPanel.h/.mm`; CHANGED `ViewController.mm` (3D-only "Cuts" button next to `:303-349`, panel constraints, `excludedViews :239-240`, reset on bid exit)
- PREREQUISITE (CI, before the shader step): `.github/workflows/build-bgfx-ios.yml` checks out bgfx / bx / bimg UNPINNED on macos-latest while `build.yml:22-24` pins SHAs and `BUILD.md:64` requires the same-commit shaderc -> pin it first (shader changes are not host-testable, the RPV-29 entry of `PORTING_BLOCKERS.md`: "not host-testable (no `shaderc`)")
- NEW tests: `Config/Tests/Settings/test_section_cut.cpp`, `Config/Tests/Meshing/test_section_cap.cpp`

**Touch adaptation.** Replace the always-visible Windows strip with a 3D-only "Cuts" button in the bottom toolbar that opens a translucent panel above it: two stacked rows on iPhone portrait (label + value line, full-width slider), max width ~480 pt anchored bottom-leading on iPhone landscape and iPad. Keep the 0..1000 range, the right end = off, the Windows labels and the feet text; add "tap the value text = off" (iOS addition). The panel is in the camera `excludedViews` so slider drags do not orbit, and the 3D long-press recogniser (`ViewController.mm:255-260`, which has no delegate and still receives touches that start on the panel) gets a delegate whose `gestureRecognizer:shouldReceiveTouch:` refuses touches inside the panel (device check: hold a slider thumb for 2 s over a takeoff, no menu opens). The cut stays active when the panel closes (the button shows the active state), is not persisted, resets when the bid is left, survives page toggles and camera Reset. The PICK pass discards the cut-away side (data safety: the 3D long-press edits a takeoff). Caps rebuild coalesced off the render thread, newest value wins. Cap outlines ship with the edge overlay and the selected-element outline. No popover needed on iPad.

**Dependencies.** No new library. The shader pin (above). The edge overlay (5.17) is built first: cap outlines reuse its line pass. The 3D page image is never cut (nothing to do until it exists). `strict_fp.hpp` must be included first in the cap translation unit. Independent of M1-M3.

**Risk.** Medium overall (the cut alone low-medium, the caps step high). The committed shader headers can only be regenerated by a manual workflow; the cap geometry port; a cut must never leave a cut-away element pickable. Windows covers the native cap code only with one pixel test (`test_renderer_section_cuts.py:151`) and two pick tests (`:123`, `:133`), all skipped off the native Windows platform (`:83-84`); the JS slicer tests are the real oracle.

**Size and order of work.** L (estimate: about 4-6 sessions). Order: (1) CI pin of the bgfx workflow; (2) `SectionCut` Core + tests; (3) cap geometry + JS-oracle tests; (4) shaders + uniform + pick pass discard; (5) panel UI; (6) caps (coalesced); (7) device pack rows (cut a project with a column, check picking across the cut).

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register Q-1 to Q-11): Q-1 `mesh_view.py:982` - `min(1.0, max(0.0, float(fraction)))` turns a NaN fraction into 0.0 (the whole scene cut away) while the renderer treats non-finite as disabled (`renderer.cpp:1365-1366`); unreachable from the slider -> ignore a non-finite fraction. Q-2 `mesh_view.py:998-1017` vs `viewer.html:2203` - a zero-width extent enables the cut in the app, the export disables the slider -> follow the export (no extent when high <= low or not finite). Q-3 `section_cut_controls.py:33` vs `viewer.html:2222-2225` - label ties: Python half-even, JS half away from zero -> half-even via `format_double`, host-test the tie 48.25 -> `48.2'` (5.16 iOS files). Q-4 `mesh_view.py:1017` (pinned) - the cut is a fraction of the CURRENT bounds, so the plane moves when pages are toggled -> kept, shown in the device checks. Q-5 `renderer.cpp:943` - nesting tests only the first vertex of the inner loop -> copied for all pinned cases (a representative point off the ring only if no pinned result changes). Q-9 caps rebuilt synchronously on every change -> coalesced off the render thread. Far-left slider cuts the whole scene (pinned `test_section_cut_controls.py:69-72`) -> kept (N-M4-19).

---

### 5.17 Whole-scene feature-edge overlay in 3D (S-M, risk medium)

**What Windows does.** Three different "feature edge" rules exist; the 3D overlay and the selection outline use the NATIVE RENDERER rule (`renderer.cpp:70-158`): weld by position at 1e-4, boundary edges plus creases over 15 degrees, no diagonals, drawn at mesh colour x 0.45 with depth test and a polygon offset on the fills, default OFF, persisted `show_edges`, redraw only (never a scene rebuild). iOS has only the DXF rule (`ost_geometry`, 0.1 rad, unwelded, identical to Windows, DXF-oracle tested) and uses it for the yellow selection outline only.

**Windows modules.**
- `cpp_extensions/src/renderer/renderer.cpp:70-158` - `feature_edge_indices` (weld 1e-4 `:89`, threshold `:114`: `cosf(15*3.14159f/180)`, no diagonals); `:447-460`, `:524-542` (`add_mesh`, line buffer built once); `:2011-2043` (`render_edges`: depth test on, depth write off, colour x 0.45, `GL_LINES`); `:2216-2256` (fills with polygon offset while edges are on); `:1976-2010` (`render_wireframe`: yellow selection outline); `:1018`, `:2529`, `:2542`, `:1237-1239` (`show_edges` default false, MSAA + `GL_LINE_SMOOTH`)
- `cpp_extensions/src/mesh/feature_edges.cpp:91-173` - rule B (directed-edge adjacency, 0.1 rad, no welding; DXF and boolean results)
- `ost_visualizer/domain/entities/config.py:9-13`, `:59`, `:139`, `:245-246`; `domain/aggregates/config_aggregate.py:95-96`, `:209-220`, `:416-428` - `show_edges` (default False, strict bool)
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/components/edges_toggle.py:6-20`, `mesh_view.py:857-873`, `ui_event_coordinator.py:691-703`, `managers/app_config_presentation_manager.py:58-62`, `dialogs/options/options_tab.py:149-151` - the 3D Edges checkbox and Options entry
- `ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/renderers/threejs/templates/viewer.html:2386-2403` - HTML export twin (15 degrees, 0.45); `tools/tower_crane_kit.py:879-922` (the only executable Python mirror of the renderer rule, double precision)

**Windows tests that pin it.** `tests/tools/test_tower_crane_kit.py` (`FeatureEdgeTests :483-506`: box 12 edges, coplanar quads no inner edges); `tests/integration/native/test_boolean_geometry.py:6-24` (rule B); `tests/domain/entities/test_config.py:17-39`, `:98`; `tests/domain/aggregates/test_config_aggregate.py:1035-1050`; `tests/presentation/components/test_edges_toggle.py:59-123` (toggling only redraws). About 3 for the edge rule + ~4 for the setting portable; roughly 8 Windows OpenGL pixel tests are not portable; NO Windows test covers the rule on an ordinary scene mesh, so most iOS tests are new with hand-derived or Python-mirror oracles.

**iOS today / iOS files touched.** `Core/Geometry/feature_edges.hpp:6-13` / `.cpp:90-172` (rule B), `Rendering/Renderer.mm:401-448` (per-mesh line buffer from rule B at every GPU upload, main thread), `:736-757` (drawn only for the selected takeoff / condition), `:23-32` (view ids), `:223-232` (scene framebuffer RGBA8 + D24S8, NO MSAA); bgfx has no depth bias / polygon offset; `Core/Domain/RenderConfig.hpp`, `ProjectStore+Conditions.cpp:749-821` (preferences pattern), `ImportService.mm:484-516` (a render-config change rebuilds all meshes), the 3D toolbar (room for one more 3D item), the Visualization Settings sheet (`DatabaseViewController.mm:815-870`, anchored). New / changed:
- NEW Core: `Core/Geometry/crease_edges.hpp/.cpp` (rule A: `crease_edge_indices`, `kCreaseDegrees`, `kWeldPrecision`), `Core/Domain/ViewOptions.hpp` (`show_edges`, `kEdgeColorScale`); rule B stays unchanged (DXF oracle-pinned)
- Store: the typed preference `load_view_options` / `update_view_options` on `_AppPreferences` is added to `ProjectStore+Settings.cpp` / `ProjectStore_Settings.hpp` (created in M2, 5.9; 4.13; key `show_edges`, "1" / "0"); no new store header
- CHANGED `Rendering/Renderer.h/.mm` (edge pass in its own view after the opaque view, depth-biased projection, LEQUAL, no depth write, mesh colour x 0.45, reuse `_progMeshId`), `ViewController.mm` (3D-only "Edges" button), `DatabaseViewController.mm` (Edges item in Visualization Settings); NEW `Services/ImportService+ViewOptions.h/.mm`
- NEW tests: `Config/Tests/Geometry/test_crease_edges.cpp`, `Settings/test_view_options.cpp`, `Persistence/test_view_options_store.cpp`

**Touch adaptation.** A 3D-only "Edges" button in the bottom toolbar (check-mark title, accessibility label; fits a 375 pt iPhone) plus an "Edges" item in the Visualization Settings sheet (the anchored iPad popover already exists), ONE persisted setting, default OFF; redraw only, via a separate notification (never `OSTRenderConfigChangedNotification`). Lines are 1 device pixel on Metal and the scene target has no MSAA (about 0.33 pt aliased on a 3x iPhone; Windows has MSAA + line smoothing): ship 1 px for parity and judge on the device. No hover or tooltip. The selection outline moves to the same rule, so its look changes (device recheck).

**Dependencies.** None hard; build BEFORE 5.16 (cuts add the clip uniform to the edge draw and the cap outlines). No new library or framework (bgfx / Metal); no shaderc for the baseline. Optional later: the HTML export Edges toggle.

**Risk.** Medium. Core low (~90 lines, deterministic, host-testable). The renderer pass is not host-testable and its first run is on a device; bgfx has no depth bias, so z-fighting needs a device-tuned projection bias; 1 px aliased lines may be too faint on 3x screens; the exact-threshold tie on 24-segment circle columns has no callable Windows oracle; it changes the shipped selection outline; main-thread extraction cost and draw-call count on large scenes are unmeasured.

**Size and order of work.** Small to medium. Order: (1) `crease_edge_indices` + `ViewOptions` tests red -> green; (2) preference store; (3) edge pass + Edges button + settings item; (4) selection outline on rule A; (5) device pack rows with the largest project (frame time, draw calls).

**Windows oddities and the safe behaviour kept** (register E-1 to E-10): E-1 `renderer.cpp:114` - pi truncated to `3.14159f`: the threshold is 0.96592587 not cos 15 = 0.96592583, and the default circle has exactly 24 segments, so each vertical line of a column is decided by the last float bit (a float32 simulation draws 8-16 of 24 lines; not authoritative) -> an exact 15 degree crease is smooth, consistently (N-M4-21; the owner may look at a circle count with Edges on in the Windows app). E-2 `renderer.cpp:122`, `:148` - `normalize` of a zero-area triangle gives NaN and silently erases the edge -> guard the zero normal and keep a boundary line. E-3 `renderer.cpp:134-149` - edges shared by 3+ faces depend on triangle order -> deterministic: draw every edge whose face count is not 2. E-4 `renderer.cpp:89`, `:101-105` - the weld is a grid hash (`llround(x*1e4)`), coincident vertices across a rounding boundary stay separate -> ported as is, pinned by a test. E-5 unordered iteration order -> sorted output. E-7 three different feature-edge rules in one app -> rule B kept for DXF, rule A added separately, documented. E-8 no transparent check (edges of transparent meshes at full intensity) -> kept; to be confirmed on the device (M4 pack). E-9 `config.py:9-13`, `config_aggregate.py:215-217` - one non-bool `show_edges` resets the whole configuration -> an invalid stored value means off for that key only.

---

## 6. M1 integration plan

The M1 build starts right after this plan is approved. Streams are roles, not people: each stream can be a separate worktree or agent; only the integration owner (S0) merges into `ios-feature-m1`.

### 6.1 Streams and file ownership (so parallel work does not collide)

| Stream | Features | New files (own them alone) |
|---|---|---|
| **S0** foundations + integration | bid lock guard with its one UI bridge, UID limit helper, text-limit helpers, popover anchors, per-feature includes / marker blocks, device pack, docs | `Core/Domain/BidLock.*`, `ProjectStore+BidLock.cpp`, `ProjectStore_BidLock.hpp`, `next_uid_within_access_limit` in `ProjectStore_Integrity.*`, `Core/Domain/TextLimits.hpp/.cpp` (the shared UTF-16 length and control-character helpers, 6.2 S0.b) with `Config/Tests/Parsers/test_text_limits.cpp`, `Services/ImportService+BidLock.h/.mm` (the ONE UI bridge of the lock check, `+ (BOOL)isBidLocked:(NSString *)bidUID`: it calls `ost_store::bid_is_locked([ImportService _dbPath], ...)` and answers YES whenever the read fails, fail closed) and `ViewController+BidLock.mm` (the helper `_refuseIfBidLocked`, which shows "This bid is locked.", 4.1; S1 to S4 call them before they open a form, alert or menu action, and none of them defines a second one), `Config/Tests/Persistence/test_bid_lock*.cpp` (and `BidLockOracleData.hpp`), `Services/ImportService_Internal.h` (the shared bridge header, 4.4 item 3), `DEVICE_CHECKS.md` (created by S0 in S0.d: it does not exist yet; as built: not created by S0, created at the end of M1, see 6.4), the empty stubs of the six per-feature store headers (below) |
| **S1** conditions | New Condition (5.1) + condition folders (5.2) | `Core/Domain/ConditionCreate.*`, `ConditionFolderRules.*`, `ProjectStore+ConditionCreate.cpp`, `ProjectStore_ConditionCreate.hpp`, `ProjectStore+ConditionFolders.cpp`, `ProjectStore_ConditionFolders.hpp`, `ImportService+ConditionCreate.*`, `ImportService+ConditionFolders.*`, `ConditionFoldersViewController.*`, `Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_create*.cpp`, `test_condition_folder_*.cpp` |
| **S2** layers | layers (5.3) | `Core/Domain/LayerEdit.*`, `ProjectStore+Layers.cpp`, `ProjectStore_Layers.hpp`, `ImportService+Layers.*`, `ViewController+LayerEditing.mm`, `Config/Tests/Persistence/test_layer_edit_*.cpp` |
| **S3** pages A | page menu, rename page (5.5), delete page (5.6) | `Core/Domain/PageRules.*`, `PageDeletion.*`, `Core/Data/PageDeletePlan.*`, `ProjectStore+PageRename.cpp`, `ProjectStore_PageRename.hpp`, `ProjectStore+PageDelete.cpp`, `ProjectStore_PageDelete.hpp`, `ImportService+PageRename.*`, `ImportService+PageDelete.*`, `ViewController+PageMenu.mm`, `ViewController+PageRename.mm`, `ViewController+PageDelete.mm`, `Config/Tests/Persistence/test_page_rename_*.cpp`, `test_delete_page*.cpp` |
| **S4** pages B | Set Scale (5.4) + undo page-scale capture + number formatters | `Core/Domain/PageScale*.*`, `Core/Domain/OverlayRect.*`, `ProjectStore+PageScale.cpp`, `ProjectStore_PageScale.hpp`, `ImportService+PageScale.*`, `PageScaleViewController.*`, `ViewController+PageScale.mm`, tests in Geometry / Formatting / Persistence / Settings |

The six per-feature store headers (`ProjectStore_ConditionCreate.hpp`, `ProjectStore_ConditionFolders.hpp`, `ProjectStore_Layers.hpp`, `ProjectStore_PageRename.hpp`, `ProjectStore_PageDelete.hpp`, `ProjectStore_PageScale.hpp`) are created by S0 in S0.d as empty stubs (declarations only: a namespace and the include guard) together with their `#include` lines; the stream named in its row then owns the header.

Shared files and who may edit them:

| Shared file | Owner | Rule for the others |
|---|---|---|
| `ProjectStore.hpp` | S0 | S0 adds ONE `#include "ProjectStore_<Feature>.hpp"` per feature in step 0 (the headers are created as empty stubs at the same time, see above) and rewrites the stale comment at `:248-255` (4.2); nobody else edits it (S0 also rewrites the stale sentence in `ConditionFormRules.hpp:105-118`, 4.2) |
| `ProjectStore_Integrity.hpp/.cpp` | S0 | read-only for the others |

| `ProjectStore+Load.cpp` | S2 (moves the `BidLayers` query of `load_project` and `read_layer_list` out of the anonymous namespace and exports both, for `reload_layers`, 5.3); S1 only if the folder load changes | |
| `ProjectStore.cpp` (`BidCascade` shares the page-table list) | S3 | |
| `EditConditionViewController/*` | S0 first (popover anchors, own commit), then S1 (create mode) | S1 rebases on S0's commit |
| `ConditionsViewController.h/.mm`, `ViewController+Conditions.mm` | S1 (the "+", "Folders", `folder_uid` per section, "Move to Folder...", "New Condition in <folder>") | S2 reloads the list through the existing `_refreshConditionsListForLayerVisibility` (`ViewController+Layers.mm:128-135`, which calls `loadConditionsFromProject:`; no header declares it, so S2 adds the declaration to its own marker block of `ViewController_Internal.h`); no edit of these files |
| `ViewController+Layers.mm` | S2 | |
| `ViewController+PageSelector.mm`, `ViewController+PageMenu.mm` | S3 (creates the menu with the rename item first) | S4 inserts the "Set Scale..." item as the FIRST item AFTER S3's rename lands (fixed order: Set Scale, Rename, Delete; as built the integrator inserted it, commit `3367239`, 4.8); nobody else creates the file |
| `ViewController_Internal.h` | each stream, in its own marker block created by S0 (one block per stream: `M1 bid lock (S0)`, `M1 conditions (S1)`, `M1 layers (S2)`, `M1 pages A (S3)` and `M1 pages B (S4)`; the S0 working tree has `// ---- M1 pages (S3, S4) ----` as one shared block, which S0 splits in S0.d). The blocks sit in the category `ViewController (Internal)`, which has no storage, so they hold METHOD declarations only: the `BOOL` property `areaPlacementInProgress` of 5.5 is NOT in a block, S0 adds it to the class extension `@interface ViewController ()` at `:26-39` | adjacent appends only; a stream declares in its block EVERY method of its categories that another file calls or names in `@selector(...)` / `addTarget:action:` (an undeclared method called from another file is a compile error under ARC; an undeclared `@selector` adds a first-party `-Wundeclared-selector` warning, which 6.3 item 4 counts as a regression: the baseline has exactly six, `BUILD.md`, paragraph "Warnings in first-party code") |
| `ViewController.mm` | S3 (only: ONE line in `planView:areaPlacementInProgress:` `:719-737` that sets the `BOOL` property `areaPlacementInProgress`, which S0 declares in the class extension of `ViewController_Internal.h` (5.5: the marker blocks sit in the category `ViewController (Internal)`, which cannot hold an ivar or a synthesised property, and the ivar-plus-accessor pattern of `ViewController.mm:44-80` would need more edits in this file), and a guard in `_handle3DLongPress:` `:520` that returns when the touch starts inside `pageBar3D` or `pageBar2D` (both bars stay visible in 3D mode); the guard is added in M1 without waiting for M1-14, because the camera recognisers already exclude the page bars (`ViewController.mm:239-240`) and this whole-view recogniser has no delegate and would run `_selectAllTakeoffsForCondition` on a long press of a page button of either bar) | nobody else edits it in M1: S3's reload is `_reloadProjectSelectingPage:` in `ViewController+PageDelete.mm` (5.6), S4 reloads through `_loadCurrentPageIntoPlanViewResetZoom:NO`, S2 rebuilds `sortedLayerUIDs` in its own category; a line in `ViewController.mm` that cannot be avoided is sent to S3; S4's undo capture needs no edit here (its two delegate methods live in `ViewController+PageScale.mm`, 5.4) |
| `UndoRedoController.h/.mm` | S4 | nobody else in M1 (`UndoHistory.hpp` is not edited); the two optional delegate methods it declares (`undoRedoPageScaleForTakeoff:pageUID:` and `undoRedoPageScaleForPage:`, 5.4) are implemented in S4's `ViewController+PageScale.mm`, not in `ViewController.mm` |
| `ProjectService.h/.mm` | S3 (page-list refresh: `refreshPageNames`, `loadProject:selectingPage:`) | S2 does not edit it: it rebuilds `sortedLayerUIDs` in `ViewController+LayerEditing.mm` through the existing `sortedLayerUIDs` and `mutableProject` properties (`ProjectService.h:24`) |
| `ProjectData.hpp` | S3 (`apply_page_name`), S4 (`apply_page_scales`), S1 only if a folder twin is kept | member additions are adjacent; merge order S3, S4, S1 |

`ImportService` is not a merge point: each feature adds its own category file (4.4; S0's own is `ImportService+BidLock.*`, 4.1), which imports S0's `ImportService_Internal.h`; S0's only edit of `ImportService.mm` is the removal of the static `OSTString` and the one `#import` of that header. CMake and `project.yml` glob the sources: no edit.

### 6.2 Build order inside M1

1. **S0.a - bid lock guard** (Core + store + tests). It must land first: every store function calls it. Streams write their tests and Core meanwhile and wire the call as soon as S0.a is on the branch (target: first days).
2. **S0.b - UID limit helper** (+ `duplicate_condition` switched, one test) and the shared text-limit helpers `ost_domain::utf16_length(const std::string& utf8)` (a `std::optional<std::size_t>`, empty for invalid UTF-8, which every writer refuses) and `ost_domain::has_control_character(const std::string& utf8)` (a `bool`; invalid UTF-8 also answers true) in `Core/Domain/TextLimits.hpp/.cpp`, with host tests in the Parsers area (ASCII, a 2-unit emoji, 127 / 128 emoji = 254 / 256 units, invalid UTF-8 reported as invalid, NUL, line breaks). N-M1-13 needs the same count in four writers (S1: condition name 255, folder name 50; S2: layer name 50; S3: page name 255) and no Core function counts UTF-16 units today, so the helper is S0's and S1 to S3 call it instead of writing their own.
3. **S0.c - popover anchors** for the seven Edit Condition pickers (own commit, own device check).
4. **S0.d - skeleton**: creates each per-feature store header (`ProjectStore_ConditionCreate.hpp`, `_ConditionFolders`, `_Layers`, `_PageRename`, `_PageDelete`, `_PageScale`) as an empty stub (declarations only: a namespace and the include guard), adds the matching `#include` lines to `ProjectStore.hpp` and hands each header to its stream (the stream then owns it); `Services/ImportService+BidLock.h/.mm` (the UI lock bridge, 4.1: `+ (BOOL)isBidLocked:(NSString *)bidUID`, YES when the read fails; the only UI entry to `ost_store::bid_is_locked`, written after S0.a so that S1 to S4 import it; the only `ImportService+<Feature>` file S0 creates: no empty stubs of the other categories, because the sources are globbed and each stream adds its own category, 4.4 item 3 and 6.1) with its helper `ViewController+BidLock.mm` (`_refuseIfBidLocked`, 4.1), `Services/ImportService_Internal.h` (the shared `OSTString` and the `_dbPath` declaration, 4.4 item 3: the file already exists in the working tree and is committed here, and `ImportService.mm` changes only by the removal of the static helper and the `#import`), the marker blocks in the category `ViewController (Internal)` of `ViewController_Internal.h` (`M1 bid lock (S0)` with the declaration of `_refuseIfBidLocked`, `M1 conditions (S1)`, `M1 layers (S2)`, and `M1 pages A (S3)` and `M1 pages B (S4)`, made by splitting the shared `M1 pages (S3, S4)` block of the working tree; the blocks hold METHOD declarations only, because a category has no storage) and the `BOOL` property `areaPlacementInProgress` in the class extension `@interface ViewController ()` of the same header (`:26-39`, 5.5), the test helpers of 6.1 (`snapshot_all`, `seed_locked_bid`, `seed_pages`), the rewrite of the two stale allocator comments (4.2), `DEVICE_CHECKS.md` (created here: it does not exist yet; it carries the 23 fixed-ui-unverified ids of `PORTING_PLAN.md:44` and the M1-xx rows; as built: not created by S0, created at the end of M1, see 6.4), the DeviceTestPack fixture list (6.4).
5. **Parallel, tests first**: S1, S2, S3, S4 each in the order of its "Order of work" (5.x): red tests -> Core -> store -> bridge -> UI. Inside S3 the page menu + rename land before delete; inside S4 the Core transform and the store can start at step 1 (pure Core), the form and undo capture later.
6. **Integration** (S0): merge by readiness (the only hard orders: S0 first; page menu before the Delete and Set Scale items), run the full host tests after each merge, keep one logical commit series per feature.
7. **Independent review per feature** against the cited Windows lines (a reviewer who did not write it); fixes; then the verification path (6.3).
8. **Device pack** built and handed over; **stop** until the owner sends the results.

### 6.3 Verification path (M1; the same path for M2 to M4)

1. Host tests on **MSVC** (Debug, Ninja: `cmake -S Config/Tests -B build/tests -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Debug`, `ctest --test-dir build/tests --output-on-failure --no-tests=error`) AND a **clang** build with fused multiply-add (the practice of the parity jobs), plus `-DOST_TEST_BOOLEAN=ON` for the full count; every new test is first run RED and the failure reason recorded.
2. **`check_objc`**: the syntax check of every touched `.m` / `.mm` / `.h` against the hand-written stub headers (baseline of job 6: 27 of 27 files, 0 errors, 4 stub warnings); the new files are added to its list. It cannot see ARC, selector or SDK-availability problems.
3. **Grep checks** on the touched Objective-C: every `UIAlertControllerStyleActionSheet` has an anchor; no `NSUserDefaults` / `UIPasteboard` for logic; no sqlite or business rule outside `Core/`.
4. **GitHub "Build IPA"** on the pushed branch (`build.yml` runs on every push): host tests 12 of 12 areas (plus any new area) on AppleClang arm64, `xcodebuild` Release against the iPhoneOS 18.5 SDK, first-party warnings compared with the baseline (9 distinct: 6 `-Wundeclared-selector`, 1 `-Wprotocol`, 2 `-Wobjc-protocol-method-implementation`; nothing added), fake-sign, IPA. Record the run id.
5. **IPA checks** (downloaded artifact, parsed locally, nothing run): bundle id `com.fabian.ostvisualizer`, `MinimumOSVersion` 16.0, thin arm64, ldid ad-hoc signature with page hashes recomputed, entitlements `get-task-allowed` only, `-Os` in the compile units, and the presence of one new string per feature in the executable (the job-6 method for proving new code is in the binary).
6. **Device pack** (6.4) and the DEVICE_CHECKS.md rows.

### 6.4 M1 device-check page (one line per check; details go to `DEVICE_CHECKS.md`)

DeviceTestPack = the set of bid files the owner imports on the device (the fixtures listed here) plus the `DEVICE_CHECKS.md` rows. Fixtures for M1: a bid with nested folders and conditions in folders; a layer in use and an unused one; a LOCKED bid (job status Locked); a bid with a page that has takeoffs and an empty page; a page with a non-12 scale (e.g. 0.25 / 8) and a takeoff; a metric bid; a bid with only one page.

| Id | Check |
|---|---|
| M1-01 | iPad only: open every Edit Condition picker (Style, Layer, Type, ...): a popover, no crash (S0.c) |
| M1-02 | New Condition: "+" opens the form; defaults of Linear / Area / Count / Attachment; Cond. No. shows "Next number: N" (= highest + 1) |
| M1-03 | Save creates exactly one condition (double tap), the row is selected and revealed, no placement starts |
| M1-04 | New Condition from a long-press in a folder lands in that folder; from the root lands at the root |
| M1-05 | New Condition in a metric bid starts with metric values; an imperial bid with imperial values |
| M1-06 | Cancel of an untouched form closes silently; Cancel of an edited form asks to discard; a 256-character name is refused |
| M1-07 | LOCKED bid: "+", Folders actions, Layers "+", Rename / Delete / Set Scale all answer "This bid is locked." and change nothing |
| M1-08 | Folders: New Folder alert, rename, "Duplicate Folder" for a sibling name, blank / unchanged silent, 51-character name refused |
| M1-09 | Folders: delete an empty folder; a folder holding a condition (or a sub-folder that does) is refused with the Windows text; deleting a parent moves sub-folders to the top level and the confirmation says so |
| M1-10 | Move to Folder from the condition long-press sheet, including "(No folder)"; the list reloads, nothing else redraws |
| M1-11 | Layers: New Layer ("+" and "New Layer Below"), rename, duplicate alert, "Comments" refused, 51-character name refused, Move Up / Down, tap still toggles visibility |
| M1-12 | Layers: delete an unused layer; a layer used by a condition is refused with the Windows text; a created layer appears in the Edit Condition layer picker at once |
| M1-13 | iPad only: Layers: alerts and menus only, no crash |
| M1-14 | Rename Page: long-press a page button; blank -> "Validation Error"; success updates the bar and the 2D / 3D chips keep the active-mode colour, selection and zoom stay; very long name; during an area placement the menu items are disabled and after it they are enabled again; in the 3D view a long press on a page button of the 3D bar AND of the 2D bar opens only the page menu: no takeoff action sheet, no select-all of the selected condition (the guard of `_handle3DLongPress:`) |
| M1-15 | Delete Page: confirmation (Cancel default); delete a page with takeoffs; the next page is selected and the 2D / 3D chips keep the active-mode colour; undo history is empty; the last page cannot be deleted |
| M1-16 | Set Scale: presets per style, custom pair, invalid text and a decimal comma refused with the Windows text, Save changes quantities but not sheet positions, zoom kept |
| M1-17 | Set Scale "apply to all pages": the confirmation appears; undo after a Set Scale replays rescaled positions (move a takeoff, change the scale, undo; delete a takeoff, change the scale, undo the delete: the takeoff is back at the same sheet position; the capture goes through the two new optional delegate methods, which no host test reaches) |
| M1-18 | After each write: close and reopen the bid, kill and relaunch the app: everything is persisted |
| M1-19 | New Condition and Folders on iPad (iPad only): the form opens as a centred sheet; the "+" and "Folders" buttons, the folder alerts and the long-press sheet (Duplicate, Delete, Move to Folder...) open anchored, no crash |
| M1-20 | Save of an untouched New Condition form shows "Condition name cannot be empty." and creates nothing |

Open item OI-M1-1: Windows acceptance of the M1 rows. It cannot be checked until M3 (the iOS database is SQLite), is closed by the M3 round trip (9.2) and is not a check of this pack, so it does not block the merge (9.1 item 8).

If no iPad is available (N-M1-26), the rows marked "iPad only" (M1-01, M1-13, M1-19) are recorded "not run: no device"; the merge rule accepts them only with the owner's written word, and the popover fix is then verified by the grep check of 6.3 item 3 and code review.

(As built: `DEVICE_CHECKS.md` at the repository root was created at the end of M1 with the 23 carried ids and the rows M1-01 to M1-20; `DeviceTestPack/` holds ten `.osp` bids, `make_pack.py` and `PACK_SHA256.txt`, and its `README.md` maps each file to the checks it serves. The pack has no page with a NULL or 0 stored scale, so the Set Scale refusal of N-M1-22 is not exercised by it, and no check opens the page menu while the Conditions or Layers sheet is up at its medium detent, where the page bars stay tappable: both are open points in 9a.7. The Windows acceptance of the pack bids is OI-M1-1, not run.) Updated 2026-11-27 (parity pass 2): `DEVICE_CHECKS.md` carries eleven more rows P2-01 to P2-11 for the screen behaviour that parity pass 2 changed, each "fixed-ui-unverified" and not run; the M1 rows were not renumbered, and the rows whose expected text changed were updated in place (9a.9).

### 6.5 M2 streams and file ownership (same rules as 6.1; written before M2 starts)

Streams: **S5** clipboard (5.7) owns `Core/Domain/Clipboard.*`, `PasteAxisSnap.hpp`, `TakeoffSelection.hpp` (pasted group), `ProjectStore+Paste.cpp` (it also holds `insert_hole` for S6), `ProjectStore_Paste.hpp`, `Components/ProjectStore+Takeoffs.cpp` and `ProjectStore_Internal.hpp` (the `insert_takeoff_in` declaration only: it loses `static`, `ProjectStore+Takeoffs.cpp:282`), `ImportService+Paste.*`, `ClipboardService.*`, `ViewController+Clipboard.mm`, `UndoRedoController.{h,mm}`, and in `ViewController.mm` the two long-press functions `_showTakeoffContextMenu:` and `planView:didLongPressEmptySpaceAtPoint:` plus the existing functions the pasted group passes through: `_selectionState`, `_applySelectionState:`, `_syncSelection`, `_handleDeleteTakeoff`, `planView:didDragTakeoff:handleIndex:canvasDelta:` and `planView:didEndDragTakeoff:` (5.7). **S6** backout (5.8) owns `Core/Geometry/backout_rules.*`, `Core/Domain/BackoutContext.hpp`, `PlanView+Backout.mm`, `ViewController+Backout.mm`, `FloatingModeButton.mm`, and adds its iPad Backout sheet item as ONE line in S5's `_showTakeoffContextMenu:` after S5's change lands, calling a method of `ViewController+Backout.mm`; in `ViewController.mm` S6 owns `_setEditMode:` and adds ONE line at its end, `[self _updateModeChips]` (implemented in `ViewController+Backout.mm`; S5's `_applySelectionState:` makes the one other call, 5.8). **S7** snap (5.9) owns `Core/Geometry/snap_index.*`, `takeoff_snap_segments.*`, `pdf_vectors.*`, `snap_source_cache.*`, `placement_snap.*`, `Core/Domain/SnapPreferences.*`, `ProjectStore+Settings.cpp`, `ProjectStore_Settings.hpp`, `ImportService+SnapSettings.*`, `PdfVectorService.*`, `PlanView+Snap.mm`, `ViewController+SnapSettings.mm`, `SnapSettingsViewController.*`; in `ViewController.mm` S7 owns one line at the top of `_refreshOverlays`, and in `PlanView.h` the one public method that line calls (5.9). File ownership is unchanged at Windows `d26893c` (A4 of job 9): the new rules of 5.7 live in S5-owned files, `Core/Domain/PasteAxisSnap.hpp` (flags, swap, pasted anchor), `Core/Domain/TakeoffSelection.hpp` (`group_uids` plus the source anchor and the pasted anchor of the group, the arming rules) and `_showTakeoffContextMenu:` (the group-member rule); S6 and S7 are not affected and S7's `PlanView.h` marker block is unchanged. M2 is not started.

Shared files: `ProjectStore.hpp` (the include lines of `ProjectStore_Paste.hpp` and `ProjectStore_Settings.hpp` are added in one commit before the streams start); `ImportService.h/.mm` (no edit in M2, categories only: each category imports S0's `ImportService_Internal.h` for `[ImportService _dbPath]` and `OSTString`, 4.4 item 3); `UndoRedoController.{h,mm}` (S5 only: S5 adds the Paste action; S7 does not edit it, the snap index is invalidated from `_refreshOverlays`, 5.9); `PlanView+Placement.mm` (S7 first replaces `_placementPointAtCanvas` by the resolver, S6 adds its backout branch after that lands and rebases); `FloatingModeButton.mm` (S6 only; S7 does not edit it. The floating button is `ViewController`'s property `fab` (`ViewController_Internal.h:34`, a subview of `self.view` created at `ViewController.mm:162-172`) and PlanView holds no reference to it (grep of `PlanView/`: no hit), so a PlanView category cannot place a chip relative to it. Both chips are therefore `ViewController` categories anchored to `fab` (`fab.centerXAnchor`, `fab.topAnchor`): S6's Backout chip on the leading half of the chip row, created and placed by `ViewController+Backout.mm`; S7's Snap chip on the trailing half, created, shown or hidden and presented by S7's `ViewController+SnapSettings.mm` (`_updateSnapChip`); `PlanView+Snap.mm` does not touch it. S6's `_updateModeChips` (5.8) calls `_updateSnapChip`, so neither stream needs the other's file); `PlanView.mm/.h`, `PlanView_Internal.h`, `ViewController.mm`, `ViewController_Internal.h` (one marker block per stream, created before the streams start, adjacent appends only; an edit inside an EXISTING function cannot be split by marker blocks, so each such function has one owner: in `ViewController.mm` S5 owns `_showTakeoffContextMenu:`, `planView:didLongPressEmptySpaceAtPoint:`, `_selectionState`, `_applySelectionState:`, `_syncSelection`, `_handleDeleteTakeoff`, `planView:didDragTakeoff:handleIndex:canvasDelta:` and `planView:didEndDragTakeoff:` (S6 adds one line to `_showTakeoffContextMenu:` after S5's change lands), S6 owns `_setEditMode:` (one line, `[self _updateModeChips]`) and S7 owns one line at the top of `_refreshOverlays`; S6 and S7 edit no other existing function. The pre-step commit that creates the marker blocks also creates `ViewController+Backout.mm` and `ViewController+SnapSettings.mm` with empty bodies of `_updateModeChips` and `_updateSnapChip`, like the empty header stubs of S0.d (6.2), so neither stream waits for the other and S5's and S6's calls compile from the first commit); `Config/Tests/README.md` and all docs (integration owner, end of M2).

## 7. NEEDS MY DECISION

Every question has a DEFAULT, so work continues while it is open, except N-M3-01, N-M3-02 and N-M3-14, where the default is a proposal that waits for your answer. **Every M1 default below is applied in the M1 build**; your answer changes it after the device results. For M2 to M4 the column "Needed" says when the answer is required. The default is the safe behaviour of section 8, except where a row says that the Windows behaviour is kept on purpose (for example N-M1-01 scope, N-M1-16, N-M1-23, N-M1-24, N-M2-08, N-M2-10, N-M2-14, N-M3-15 for P-5 and P-11 to P-13, N-M4-07, N-M4-19). Where a Windows file:line is given, the question comes from an oddity of section 8.

### 7.2 M2 (answer before M2 starts unless noted)

| Id | Question | DEFAULT | Alternatives | Needed |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| N-M2-01 | Where do Copy, Duplicate and Paste live on touch? | Items of the existing takeoff long-press sheet (Copy, Duplicate, and "Copy all of this condition on this page", an iOS-only convenience that Windows does not have); "Paste Here" in the empty-space long-press (one sheet with "Select All of Condition" when a condition is highlighted); no toolbar buttons; iPad C / V / D key commands at the end. | `UIEditMenuInteraction` (iOS 16, unproven in this app) | before M2 |

| N-M2-03 | Paste target, Duplicate offset, Intelligent Paste. | Paste at the pressed point (source anchor to the point), viewport centre when none; Duplicate = an iOS touch adaptation: the copy lands at the plain offset (snap increment, 1.0 if off), is selected and draggable. Windows with Intelligent Paste on (its default, `config.py:68`) drops a Duplicate at the pointer or viewport centre with a pending drag, and uses the plain offset only with Intelligent Paste off or without an anchor (`plan_view_action_handler.py:5330-5349`); Intelligent Paste always on. Clarified at Windows d26893c (`df78f67`), no default value changed: the guided drag (8 pt screen-point axis guides) is armed after "Paste Here" AND after Duplicate (Windows arms it whenever intelligent paste is on and an anchor exists; a Duplicate on iOS lands at the plain offset, so its pasted anchor is source + offset); the drag anchor is the pasted anchor of the item that gave the source anchor; the guide flags swap on 90 / 270 pages; the guided axis is not rounded to the snap increment (CB-12: the rule is now the Windows rule too, fixed at `d1c430a` and written out in 5.7 item (5) for the group, the single item and the one-point handle; clarification only, no default value changed, M2 NOT started). | A preference screen | before M2 |
| N-M2-04 | Selection after paste. | Only a "pasted group" that the next drag moves (no marquee, no Select All). Clarified at Windows d26893c (`df78f67` keeps the multi-selection when the pressed item is already selected): a long press on a member of the group keeps the group and Copy / Duplicate act on the whole group, Delete (toolbar) removes group plus descendants, a long press on a non-member clears the group and acts on that takeoff alone. RESOLVED (owner decision 3, 2026-11-28): "Count as Negative" applies to the pasted group as on Windows; a single takeoff stays as UND-09 (the pressed takeoff only). The Windows "group" is the current multi-selection (after Paste the pasted takeoffs, children included, stay selected; annotation and unknown uids are dropped, no descendant expansion). Rule (`plan_view_action_handler.py:2134-2181`, `input_handler.py:3247-3251`, `view_context_menu.py:162-196`, `takeoff.py:136`): the item is offered only if the group has at least one root takeoff and NO child takeoff (a hole or an attachment: the pasted area with its hole has no item); it is checked only if EVERY takeoff of the group is negative; the new value is "not all negative" for EVERY takeoff of the group (not the value of the pressed item); ONE undo entry holds old and new of ALL members, including those already at the target. The iOS Core function `negative_toggle_for_press(takeoffs, group_uids, pressed_uid)` (`Core/Domain/NegativeChange.hpp`, built in job 11, suite `Und09_NegativeGroup`) implements it; the single path (empty group, a group of one, a pressed takeoff that is not a member) is the old behaviour. iOS has no multi-selection before M2, so the ObjC handler passes an empty group today: the M2 paste wiring passes the pasted takeoff uids as the group (5.7). STILL OPEN (small; default: no new item, the toolbar Delete stays): should the long-press sheet also get a Delete item (the Windows menu has one)? | Full multi-select (touches hit-testing, handles, delete: a separate feature) | before M2 |

---

## 8. Windows oddities register

The owner asked to be told about every Windows behaviour that looks accidental, inconsistent or unsafe. Rule of this work: the SAFE behaviour is kept in iOS (not the Windows one) and the case is listed here with the Windows file:line (Windows `49a2d59`; paths relative to the Windows repo root). All rows come from reading the code and tests; "not run" cases are marked "(inferred)", "(reasoned, not run)", "(derived, not seen on screen)", "(unverified)" or "(needs a Windows run to confirm)", and uncertain readings carry "(low confidence)" or "(moderate confidence)". Rows marked "(pinned)" are behaviours a Windows test pins: they are kept on purpose and only documented. After each milestone the rows move into `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json` / `PORTING_BLOCKERS.md` ("possible Windows defects") as in earlier jobs. Updated 2026-11-27 (parity pass 2): the M1 and M2 tables have a last column "At Windows d26893c" (Windows HEAD `d26893c`; "unchanged" = no behaviour hunk in `49a2d59..d26893c`; "FIXED" = Windows fixed it; "converged" = iOS now behaves the same; history of the other columns is kept), and the M3 and M4 sections carry short "changed on Windows" notes. Observations that change nothing (dead code, docstrings) are left out. Updated 2026-11-27 (parity pass 3): Windows HEAD is now `d1c430a` (one commit on top of `d26893c`); a cell of the last column that names `d1c430a` supersedes the `d26893c` reading, every other cell is unchanged (the commit changed NC-6, LY-15 (new), DP-5, CB-12 and the three notes under the registers).

### M1

| Id | Windows file:line | What Windows does | Safe behaviour kept | At Windows d26893c |
|---|---|---|---|---|

No. "0", refuses it (< 1), then creation drops the typed number (MAX+1 wins) | read-only next number | FIXED (`0a24294`): the form shows max(ref_no) + 1, read-only (`condition_action_handler.py:619-633`, `edit_condition_dialog.py:420`). iOS: converged since parity pass 2 (no floor at 0, `c4673b6`). |
