(As built: 9a.5 collects the rows the builders applied, with the Windows file:line; the build found further cases that are not rows above, and 9a.6 lists them with the ids the builders used: NC-8b, LY-4b, RP-1b, CF-1b, SS-10 addendum and others.)

Parity pass 2 (Windows d26893c): 21 rows of the register are FIXED on Windows (NC-1, NC-2, NC-3, NC-5, NC-6, NC-10, CF-1, CF-2, CF-7, CF-8, LY-1, LY-2, LY-3, SS-2, SS-3, SS-5, SS-10, RP-2, RP-3, DP-2, DP-5) and 3 CHANGED differently (CF-5, LY-6, LY-11); the other 48 are unchanged and their defects are still present (the iOS safe behaviour stands). Beside the register ids Windows changed, with these ids of the analysts (not rows above): the M1 text-limit row (`M1-TEXTLIMITS`: Windows added ONE limit, page names over 255 characters, RP-2; condition names, folder names, layer names and control characters are still unchecked on Windows); the committed-but-not-refreshed outcomes of layer rename / move / delete (LY-UI-4, same behaviour as iOS); A9-X1 (the metric width, above, possible Windows defect, not copied); A9-X2 (Show All / Hide All now touch only the bid's own layers and keep memory equal to a reload; with no own layers the sidebar says "This bid has no layers of its own to show or hide.": ported as the "Layer Visibility" alert); A9-X3 (Renumber and Duplicate say what happened, no iOS Renumber command); A9-X4 (a page scale save that raises in the Windows main window now shows "The page scale could not be saved, so the previous scale is kept." under "Set Scale"; iOS always alerts); A9-X5 (the summary type rule, M3 input, see the M3 notes). Windows defects still present at `d26893c` and not copied: LY-4 (a batch layer delete commits the earlier deletes), LY-5, LY-7 to LY-10, LY-13, LY-14, NC-4, NC-7, NC-8, NC-9, NC-12, CF-4, CF-6, CF-10 (the recursive tree build), CF-12, SS-1, SS-11, SS-12, RP-7 to RP-9, DP-1, DP-3, DP-4, DP-6, DP-8, DP-9, a sidebar layer move or rename that returns False is silent, the undo / redo replay has no range guard, the SQL Server sidebar delete uses the in-memory usage rule, and Windows blocks a stored quantity that the style does not offer from being cleared with "(none)" (`edit_condition_dialog.py:1913-1925`).

Parity pass 3 (Windows d1c430a) changed these statements of the paragraph above: FIXED on Windows now: A9-X1 / NC-6 tail (the metric width), the stored quantity that the style does not offer can be cleared with "(none)" again (it is no longer a Windows defect), the stale-undo guard (the replay of a stale or failed step is refused and the entry stays; two capture-time defects remain: `plan_view_action_handler.py:1577` raises a plain ValueError and `:1707-1710` `_page_scale_for_page_uid` still answers (1.0, 1.0) for a missing page when capturing), the last-page pseudo page (DP-5, WD-081) and the winding of the 3D meshes (EXP-13, EXP-M02, GEO-15, WD-089; EXP-17 stays for the triangle ORDER only). Possible Windows weakness NEW in pass 3: LY-15 (`update_layer_show` without a scope guard). The SQL Server layer-usage count now also counts a layer used by a condition without takeoffs (`project_write_service.py`, `test_layer_delete_usage.py`); iOS is Access only and already counts `BidConditions.BidLayerUID` without takeoffs (`ProjectStore+Layers.cpp:551-596`): nothing to port; the register row LY-3 (SQL Server in-memory rule) was not re-read beyond this. Owner questions closed by pass 3: see 9a.10.

Windows d26893c (parity pass 2; M2 is NOT started and its scope list is unchanged): only commit `df78f67` changes M2 behaviour (the paste drag and the context-menu Delete, 5.7). The other ten commits of `49a2d59..d26893c` move texts into `presentation/texts.py` / `shared/texts.py` with the same strings and route errors through `show_error` / `show_warning`; `placement_mode.py`, `geometry_utils.py`, `drag_handler.py`, `cpp_extensions/src/snap`, `cpp_extensions/src/pdf`, `snap_preferences_dto.py`, `config.py`, `page_scale_transform.py`, `identity_allocation.py`, `bid_owned_identity.py`, `takeoff.py` and `selection_clipboard_service.py` are byte-identical, so every Windows line number of this table for such a file is still exact; the shifted ones are written in the last column. CB-3's second half is the only oddity of this table that Windows fixed; CB-12 (new) and the BO-12 clarification were added; "possible Windows defect, not copied": CB-12 (FIXED on Windows at `d1c430a`, parity pass 3: now a rule and an M2 input, 5.7 item (5); M2 is still NOT started). The Windows-only parts of `df78f67` (SQL Server pastes arm the guided drag, the Conditions-sidebar scrolling, the ink-header skip of annotation paste, which is M4) have no iOS counterpart. What M1 already delivered for M2: the CB-8 refusal in `rescale_entry_positions`, `refuse_new_condition_numbers` and `allocate_condition_identities(count)`, and `placement_tap_accepted` for CB-5 (5.7, Dependencies).

Changed on Windows at d26893c (parity pass 2; M3 is NOT started, the scope is unchanged; these are inputs for the build). (1) Summary type rule (C-11, A9-X5): a condition WITHOUT a type reads "(unassigned)" in the Summary row, its type group and the CSV; a type uid that names no `CdnTypes` row keeps "Unknown"; derive "no type" from the empty uid, never from the name "Unknown" (`condition_summary_service.py:446-447`). (2) Export wording: the prefix "Error creating <format> export:" is gone from every export alert, the message is the result sentence only (Summary CSV, OST, OSP and PDF; the Windows texts are quoted in 5.10); the collision note of 5.10 is reworded; the progress frame is "Exporting: <name>..." (it was "Exporting <name>...", also "Importing: <name>...", "Database setup: ...", "Duplicating: ...", "Pasting: ...") and the OST / OSP steps keep their text ("Building OST", "Writing metadata", "Collecting images", "Collecting <name>", "Packaging archive", "Writing OST"). (3) Import wording: an OST without a bid reads "The OST file contains no bid, so it cannot be imported.", an invalid takeoff graph "Some takeoffs in the OST file are invalid, so it cannot be imported.", an OSP format error "The OSP file is damaged or uses an unsupported format, so it cannot be imported."; iOS showed the technical reasons until job 11. DECIDED on 2026-11-28 (job 11, owner decision 1): iOS adopted the Windows wording of every import and export alert, one user sentence and the technical reason only in the log; the earlier decision of 2026-11-19 (keep the iOS texts) is withdrawn; the rule for M3 is section 4.15 and the Core module `FailureText` exists. (4) SS-10: the legend bytes differ between a legend rewritten by iOS (raw UTF-8) and by Windows (latin-1 plus `&#N;`); the M3 round trip must compare parsed legends, not bytes. (5) The viewer.html button texts of the HTML export are Title Case at d26893c ("Reset View", "Show All", "Hide All", "Fit Page"); the iOS HTML template has no buttons, so there is no counterpart unless M4 adds section cuts to it.

Changed on Windows at d1c430a (parity pass 3; M3 is NOT started, the scope is unchanged; inputs for the build). (1) Annotation text storage (`domain/services/annotation_text_storage.py`, `serialization.py`): an Access database stores annotation text and callout text as latin-1, exactly U+0000..U+00FF are storable (NOT cp1252: the euro sign, curly quotes, dashes and emoji are refused), checked for text and callout only and for Access only. The OST / OSP round trip of M3 must keep that limit: a Windows Access file must never receive text outside latin-1, and any annotation text that iOS later writes into a bid that goes to Windows must obey it (the refusal text is in the M4 note). It is a different mechanism from the legends (SS-10: Windows writes latin-1 plus `&#N;` references, iOS raw UTF-8; compare parsed legends, not bytes) and from X-5 (`BidTexts.Name` / `BidCallOuts.Name`). (2) Winding: Windows now builds every 3D shape outward (`domain/mesh.py:62` `outward_faces` on every generator, no flip in the boolean pass or the OBJ writer, FBX in mesh order), which is what iOS already stored; the OBJ and HTML writers of M3 therefore need no winding change and iOS has the pins of pass 3 (signed volume > 0 for 14 shapes x 3 scenes through the view meshes, the HTML export and the OBJ file; 2 plain and 3 Manifold-only tests). The bounding-box shortcut of Windows still differs (an uncut positive is rebuilt by the difference on iOS: same geometry, the triangle ORDER may differ; EXP-17 stays wont-fix). (3) Lighting of the HTML export (RPV-29, kept as iOS): Windows lights with vertex normals (nz >= 0 forced, 60 degree smoothing, no view flip) and the outward windings fix the side faces; the iOS HTML writer (`Core/Export/threejs_writer.cpp`) uses a viewer-facing flat normal and the 3D view flips the normal toward the viewer: for closed outward meshes both light the visible side from the side facing the light; only back faces, undersides and smoothing differ (MSH-21, EXP-20 stay). Nothing to port; look at it on a device when the HTML export is built.

Changed on Windows at d26893c (parity pass 2; M4 is NOT started, the scope is unchanged). None of the rows above changed. Notes: (1) the annotation caption texts ("cu yd", "{rise}/{run} Pitch", "{n} mm", "Wall Area") moved to `shared/texts.py` with identical values, so 5.12 and 5.13 keep their text; the section-cut and edge labels ("Edges", "Draw object edges in the 3D view and in HTML exports.") also kept their text. (2) Case-only changes of messages that iOS copies and that the annotation inserts reuse: "A takeoff and its parent must belong to the same page." and "<table> mutation targets do not belong to one authoritative bid." (iOS adopted both in parity pass 2), "This OST database does not support condition folder hierarchy persistence." (iOS keeps the capitalised text, a log-grade message). (3) Detached annotation / view windows (out of scope): Windows now refuses with an error when a USER navigation or window toggle cannot show its page and only logs on restore or refresh; when hotlinks and named views arrive in M4 as an in-page target, a navigation that cannot show its target should show a message only when the user triggered it. (4) `notes_tags` and the tower-crane sentences that changed parse `[crane ...]` tags in CONDITION notes, not annotations: not an M4 item, and the tower crane is out of scope. (5) The HTML export of the 3D view uses Title Case button texts at `d26893c` ("Reset View", "Show All", "Hide All", "Fit Page", "Cut north-south" with a hyphen): use them if M4 adds section cuts to the HTML export.

Changed on Windows at d1c430a (parity pass 3; M4 is NOT started, the scope is unchanged; this is an input for the annotation writers). Annotation text (and callout text) is stored in Access as latin-1: only U+0000..U+00FF can be saved (NOT cp1252: the euro sign, curly quotes, dashes and emoji are refused). The rule applies to annotation text and callout text only and only to an Access file. The first offending character is named; the alert title is "Annotation Text" and the text is `The annotation text was not saved because this database cannot store the character "€" (U+20AC). Remove or replace that character, then try again.` (the character and the code point are those of the first offence). iOS has no annotations yet; the iOS insert and edit of annotation text must refuse with this title and text before any write, because a file that holds text outside latin-1 would fail in the Windows import (X-5 is the same family: one other character fails the whole import). Windows source: `domain/services/annotation_text_storage.py`, `serialization.py`.

## 9. Definition of done per milestone

A milestone is ready for the owner's device pack only when every item of 9.1 holds for it AND the extra items of its own list in 9.2. Nothing is described as built, tested or verified before the evidence exists; a statement names what ran (host tests, CI run id, IPA parse, device).

### 9.1 For every milestone

1. **Tests before and after**: the count before (M1: 1760, 1769 with Manifold) and after is recorded per area and in `Config/Tests/README.md`. Every new test was written FIRST and run RED for the right reason (the failure text is kept in `PORTING_LOG.md`); no tolerance was relaxed to make a port pass. Each ported Windows test names its Windows file:line; oracle tables come from the READ-ONLY Windows tree (real Windows function where callable) with their file:line.
2. **Host tests green** on MSVC (Debug), on a clang build with fused multiply-add and with `-DOST_TEST_BOOLEAN=ON`; new areas appear in the README table.
3. **Independent reviewer** (who did not write the change) checked each feature against the cited Windows lines; findings fixed; the review is recorded per feature.
4. **CI green on the branch**: GitHub "Build IPA" on the head commit (run id recorded), host tests 12 of 12 areas (plus new areas) on AppleClang, `xcodebuild` Release against the iPhoneOS SDK, first-party warnings = the baseline set with nothing added, fake-sign, IPA upload. A docs-only commit carries `[skip ci]` and the last CI-verified code state is named.
5. **IPA checks** (downloaded artifact, parsed locally, nothing executed): bundle id `com.fabian.ostvisualizer`; `MinimumOSVersion` 16.0; thin arm64; ldid ad-hoc signature with all page hashes recomputed; entitlements `get-task-allowed` only (no memory entitlement); `-Os` in the compile units; at least one new string per feature present in the executable.
6. **DeviceTestPack and `DEVICE_CHECKS.md`**: fixtures and one row per check (M1: 6.4) handed to the owner; the 23 carried fixed-ui-unverified checks of `PORTING_PLAN.md` section 3 stay in the pack until verified; **work then stops** until the owner sends the results.
7. **Docs updated** at the end of the milestone (by the integration owner only): `PORTING_BLOCKERS.md` (the "Windows features without an iOS counterpart" list loses the milestone's features), `PORTING_MAP.md`, `PARITY_MAP.md`, `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json` (the register rows of section 8 as possible Windows defects with file:line), `PORTING_STATUS.*`, `PORTING_LOG.md`, `Config/Tests/README.md`, `BUILD.md` when CI changes. (As built for M1: `PORTING_PLAN.md` was updated too, and the new `DEVICE_CHECKS.md` written; the older passages of every document keep their wording as history and carry an `Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1)` marker where M1 changed the statement.)
8. **Merge only on the owner's device results** (Updated 2026-11-28 (job 11): the owner waived this rule for the merge of `ios-parity-sync-2` into `main`: all 67 device rows are recorded as "pending, merged unverified", never as Pass, the owner sends the results later and fixes go on a NEW branch from `main`; the waiver is given for that merge only, and the branch is merged under it as the last step of job 11, see 9a.11): `ios-feature-mN` is merged into `main` only when every check of the pack is recorded PASS (a blank or failed check blocks the merge; open items that the plan marks as "closed in a later milestone", such as OI-M1-1, do not block it); a status moves to "verified on device" only when EVERY check that covers it is PASS (the job-7 rule). The next milestone branches from the updated `main`.

### 9.2 Extra items per milestone

| Milestone | Extra items |
|---|---|
| **M1** | The bid lock guard has its own tests and a locked-bid fixture; the shared text-limit helpers have their own host tests; the seven Edit Condition pickers verified as popovers on an iPad; each write verified persisted after relaunch; Set Scale: whole-database rollback tests, memory == reload agreement, undo replay after a scale change; Delete Page: the schema-driven catalog test is in; the Windows-side acceptance of M1 rows is explicitly listed as OPEN until M3 (OI-M1-1, 6.4) |
| **M2** | Clipboard survives a change of bid; paste redo after a Set Scale (rescaled positions); holes re-validated at the write boundary; the snap index host-tested to the Windows corpus, and a DEVICE check that PDF-line snap points sit on the drawn lines of a rotated and a cropped sheet; M3 round-trip fixtures started with the pasted / hole rows; the M2 device-check page (rows M2-01 ...) is written at the start of M2 from the Risk blocks of 5.7-5.9 and handed over with these DeviceTestPack fixtures: a bid with a parent area holding a hole, an attachment and a count; two bids of one project; a bid whose layer of a condition is hidden; a rotated sheet and a cropped sheet with PDF lines; an iPad |
| **M3** | ROUND TRIP: an iOS export of a bid that contains rows authored in M1 and M2 (conditions, folders, layers, renamed / scaled pages, pasted takeoffs, holes) re-imports on iOS equal to the source (the Employees columns scrubbed by N-M3-10 excepted) and passes the read-only Windows in-memory acceptance run; `expand.exe` opens the OSP; the owner's desktop check (OSP sent to the PC, File > Import, pages / conditions / takeoff count / one quantity compared: N-M3-14); QPDF spike result recorded before any PDF code is merged; the PDF opens in Bluebeam and Preview on the owner's devices; share-sheet delivery, Cancel and the 2 GiB refusal checked; the CSV golden files compared with Windows output |
| **M4** | The bgfx workflow is pinned before any shader change; frame time and draw calls on the largest project with Edges and a Cut on; the owner's Windows answer on the rotated-page overlay (N-M4-15) is recorded before the move step; annotation value forms proven by an M3 export round trip; the PDF extension of N-M3-17 verified (the PDF of a page with annotations and an overlay opens in Bluebeam and Preview); Set Scale / Delete Page / layer delete verified on a bid that has annotations, named views, hotlinks and an overlay |

## 9a. M1 implementation status

Written after the M1 build (branch `ios-feature-m1`, head `87cdf8c` when this was written; the host-test runs were made on `17b3a1a`, the commit before it, see 9a.2 and 9a.3). It says what exists, what ran and what did not. It makes no statement about device behaviour: nothing of the Objective-C(++) of M1 has run on a device. The CI build of the branch is GitHub run 45528720676 on the head `2eae9ed` (the code was last changed at `87cdf8c`; the three commits after it, `85fbeac`, `a326cd4` and `2eae9ed`, are documents plus one comment of a test file): success on the first attempt, and the check of its IPA passed (9a.3 item 9; 9.1 items 4 and 5 in 9a.8 are done). The branch is not merged into `main` (no pull request) (Updated 2026-11-28 (job 11): the merge of `ios-parity-sync-2` into `main` (pull request, merge commit, tag `main-before-m1` at `06d8d69`) is the last step of job 11, authorised by the owner under the waiver of the device results; the merge commit id and the `main` run id are in `git log` and the final report, not in this file, see 9a.11). Updated 2026-11-27 (parity pass 2): the M1 code changed after this text was written (branch `ios-parity-sync-2`, code head `368ae99`); 9a.1 to 9a.8 are as of `87cdf8c` unless a place says "Updated 2026-11-27", and 9a.9 lists what changed, with the new counts.

### 9a.2 Commit and merge structure, test counts

- `git log --oneline 06d8d69..HEAD`: 79 commits at the code head `87cdf8c` (78 up to `17b3a1a`; the documents commits that follow are not counted), 10 of them merges. Order: the first four S0 commits (`090a996` skeleton, `46b4e61` lock guard, `79dd5e7` UID limit, `54ff58e` popover anchors); `1e90985` this plan; four more S0 commits (`6bd1303` DeviceTestPack, `e05a4ab` TextLimits, `ee66653` UI lock bridge, `eca80ae` marker blocks and the page-menu flag); then six Core, store and bridge chains, each built on its own branch, with its tests first, and merged in this order: New Condition `bb8a44e`, folders `39ab6ef`, layers `e022c03`, rename page `2db86ca`, delete page `7ac723e`, Set Scale `6207367`; then four Objective-C UI chains merged on top of that: conditions and folders `27c60c8`, layers `abebe26`, page menu, rename and delete `73277a4`, Set Scale `b2dde8b`; then the integrator's `3367239` (Set Scale as the first item of the 2D page menu) and `deb24ae` (starting a placement with another condition resets the area-placement flag that the page menu reads); then the fixes of the whole-milestone review: `a6dc7f9`, `3b01dc1`, `1f6bfaf`, `fb2f9bd`, `17b3a1a`, and `87cdf8c` (the folder name alerts select with `selectedTextRange`, found by the independent recheck of those fixes).
- Host tests (MSVC Debug; the clang run agrees; both runs were made on `17b3a1a`, and the code head `87cdf8c` changed only `Controllers/ConditionFoldersViewController.mm`, an Objective-C file that no host test covers, so no count can differ at the head): baseline 1760 (1769 with Manifold) -> S0 1857 -> the six Core features merged (`6207367`) 2785 -> final tip 2907 (2916 with Manifold); 0 warnings in `Config/Tests`. ### 9a.3 What was verified, and what was not

Verified (it ran):

1. **Host tests** on MSVC (Debug) and on a clang build with fused multiply-add (`-Os -mfma -ffp-contract=on`): 2907 of 2907 on `17b3a1a`, both; 2916 of 2916 with Manifold (MSVC). The chains wrote their tests first and recorded red runs; the cases their reports say could not be watched red are the Layers rename snapshot test (it composes existing rules), the New Condition notes rule (written together with its tests, covered by a mutant instead) and the removal of the second `folder_context_uid` overload.
2. **`check_objc`** (syntax against hand-written stubs; run again on the code head `87cdf8c`): 42 of 42 platform files OK, the 4 baseline stub warnings, no error. It cannot see ARC, selectors or SDK availability.
3. **The undeclared-selector scan** (a zig clang run over the 42 files; run again on `87cdf8c`, unchanged): exactly the baseline six undeclared selectors (`_elevTypeChanged:`, `_handleConditionsToggle`, `_handleLayerToggle`, `_handleExport`, `_handlePlaceTap:`, `_handlePlacePan:`), plus the scan's other classes of warning that the baseline also has (2 category-implements, `-Wprotocol` for `EditConditionViewController`), and one `-Wselector` for `_layersAddTapped:` (`ViewController+Layers.mm:63`: declared in a marker block, implemented in `ViewController+LayerEditing.mm`, another file of the same class). Whether the real `xcodebuild` reports that one was left to the CI run, with the baseline of 9 distinct first-party warnings (6.3 item 4) as the authority: run 45528720676 printed 9 distinct first-party warnings, identical to the baseline and none for `_layersAddTapped:` (item 9).
4. **Mutation checks** on scratch copies (not part of the plan's verification path): New Condition 51 mutants in the last phase (48 killed, 3 equivalent) after an earlier pass of 114 (107 killed, 7 equivalent); folders 65 (all killed, 2 more judged equivalent and not run); layers 69 (62 killed, 4 equivalent, 3 survivors that new tests now kill); rename 39 (37 killed, 2 equivalent); delete page 45 (43 killed, 2 equivalent); Set Scale 106 (98 killed, 8 equivalent). The survivors that were real became tests.
5. **Independent review**: every chain had a reviewer who did not write it (verdicts in 9a.1); then five reviewers read the whole milestone through five lenses (conditions UI, Set Scale and undo, the data path of the six writers, UIKit / ARC / threading of layers and pages, cross-file integrity): no blocker, no crash path; 22 findings, all minor after verification (one was filed as major and verified as minor: the swipe-down guard of the New Condition form did not notice typing). Eight were fixed in five commits (`a6dc7f9` to `17b3a1a`): typing in the New Condition form, an exact-bytes name conversion (a NUL), the context-menu alerts of the folder manager, a second tap on "Folders", the Rename Layer selection, Cancel of the Set Scale form, the six layer functions that could throw, stale comments. The other 14 are recorded in 9a.7. A further reviewer, who did not write the fixes, then rechecked them: ACCEPT, no blocker and no major. It found three minor residuals: the folder name alerts still used `selectAll:` (a ninth fix, `87cdf8c`: they now set `selectedTextRange` like the Rename Page and Rename Layer alerts, so no alert in `Controllers/` or `Services/` calls `selectAll:` any more), no committed test of the function-try-blocks of the six layer functions (recorded, item 6), and `+[UIAction actionWithHandler:]`, which no file compiled against the real SDK before M1 (five uses in M1; run 45528720676 has since compiled it, with the rest of the Objective-C(++) of M1, with no error: item 9). So nine fixes were made, in six commits.
6. **An allocation-failure probe** of the six layer functions on a scratch copy (a global `operator new` that fails the n-th allocation, zig clang): with the fix 12 sweeps, no exception escaped, the database stayed byte-identical; before it every injected failure escaped. Not committed: it needs its own test binary (a global `operator new` replacement) and cannot run on MSVC Debug. So the function-try-blocks of the six layer store functions (`17b3a1a`) are covered only by this probe, which is not committed: a regression there would not fail any host test (the second residual of the recheck in item 5).
7. **Hygiene**: `git diff --check` clean over the range, every changed text blob pure LF (the ten binary `.osp` fixtures excepted), no private data in the diff.
8. **The device pack** was checked on the host: the ten `.osp` files unpack with `expand.exe`, the Core import imports all ten with the counts, flags, scales and lock status asserted, two PDF engines open every PDF (`DeviceTestPack/README.md`).
9. **The GitHub build and the IPA check** (Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1); items 1 to 8 were written before the run). GitHub Actions "Build IPA", run 45528720676 on the head `2eae9ed` of `ios-feature-m1` (the code was last changed at `87cdf8c`; the three commits after it are documents plus one comment of a test file): success on the first attempt (the only run of the branch so far), created 2026-11-25 01:50 UTC (2026-11-24 20:50 New York time), finished 02:02 UTC (about 12 minutes). Every step succeeded, including "Unit tests (host build)" (ctest: 100% tests passed out of 12 test executables; the CI host tests run without Manifold), "Verify optimisation level and deployment target" (`GCC_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL=s`, i.e. `-Os`, and `IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET=16.0`, for Debug and Release), "Build app (Release, iphoneos, unsigned)", "Locate and check the app bundle", "Fake-sign with ldid", "Verify entitlements", "Package IPA" and "Upload IPA"; no error line in the log. So the Objective-C(++) of M1 compiles against the real iPhoneOS 18.5 SDK, for the first time against a real SDK. That compile is the comparison for the compile-time facts that the `check_objc` stubs could not see (selectors, types, availability); ARC and behaviour at run time are still unverified until a device run. First-party warnings of the build (not the vendored `sqlite3.c`): 9 distinct, identical to the baseline and no new one: 6 `-Wundeclared-selector` (`_elevTypeChanged:` in `EditConditionViewController+TableView.mm`; `_handleConditionsToggle`, `_handleLayerToggle` and `_handleExport` in `ViewController.mm`; `_handlePlaceTap:` and `_handlePlacePan:` in `PlanView.mm`), 2 category-implements warnings (`EditConditionViewController+TableView.mm`) and 1 `-Wprotocol` (`EditConditionViewController` does not conform to `UITableViewDataSource`); the other warnings are the toolchain ones of the earlier runs (the `ld` duplicate `-lc++`, interface orientations, `LSSupportsOpeningDocumentsInPlace`) and the vendored `sqlite3.c` ones. The IPA of the run was downloaded and checked with the IPA verifier script (nothing from the IPA was run): ALL CHECKS PASSED. Bundle id `com.fabian.ostvisualizer`; `MinimumOSVersion` 16.0; thin arm64 Mach-O, minos 16.0.0, SDK 18.5.0; 3 files in the archive (the verifier counts 5 zip members, as it did for run 45039125909); executable 5,656,448 bytes (the executable of main's run 45039125909 had 5,140,192 bytes: +516,256 bytes, +10.0%); ad-hoc ldid-style signature (SuperBlob; both CodeDirectories, all 1364 code page hashes match); entitlements `get-task-allowed` only; no memory entitlements; no `*.sc` shader sources; `-Os` from the CI-logged build settings (it cannot be read from a binary). One new string per feature, read from the bytes of the executable and absent from main's executable: "Keep Editing" (New Condition), "No folders. Tap + to add one." (condition folders), "New Layer Below" (layers), "Set Scale..." and "Custom scale values must be positive." (Set Scale), "Rename Page..." and "Page name cannot be empty." (Rename Page), "Cannot delete the last page in the bid." (Delete Page), "This bid is locked." (bid lock). So the last CI-verified code state of the branch is the head `2eae9ed` of this run, and that of `main` stays run 45039125909 (head `06d8d69`); the commit that records this carries `[skip ci]` (documents only), so this run stays the last run of the branch.

NOT verified:

- **Everything on a device.** No Objective-C(++) of M1 has run: alerts presented from swipe, menu and button handlers, `UIButton.menu` long press next to the tap, the arbitration between the page menu and the whole-view long-press recogniser, `modalInPresentation` set at run time on a form sheet inside a navigation controller, the keyboard room of the Set Scale form, a decimal comma in a comma locale, the pull-down menu with 17 presets, text selection in alert fields, `UIDeferredMenuElement` with an uncached provider (iOS 15, deployment target 16.0), the two optional undo delegate methods reaching the controller at run time, popovers on an iPad, persistence after relaunch. These are the checks M1-01 to M1-20 and the 23 carried checks.
- **The real SDK at run time.** `check_objc` uses hand-written stubs (extended during M1 for the new UIKit calls); the CI compile of run 45528720676 is the comparison with the real SDK for the compile-time facts (selectors, types, availability: no error, no new first-party warning, item 9). ARC at run time and every behaviour against UIKit stay unverified until a device run.
- **Windows acceptance of the rows M1 writes** (OI-M1-1): not before the M3 round trip.
- **AppleClang itself** (the clang runs used zig clang; the Manifold variant ran on MSVC only; the CI host step runs the Manifold stub). Windows behaviour was executed only as read-only Python oracles in memory; the Access-specific failures (names over 50 characters, Python recursion on deep chains) are inferred from reading.

One table for the six features, collected from the builders' reports. "Why" names the decision or the rule of section 1: the safe behaviour is kept and the Windows one is not copied (rule 2), a touch adaptation, or an iOS model difference. Rows with a register id are the rows of section 8. Windows behaviours that were kept on purpose are listed after the table.

| Lock (all writers) | `active_bid_write_guard.py:7-36`; `project_write_service.py:701-704` | guards the active bid in the service layer, refuses silently | every writer re-checks inside its transaction after the ownership checks, fail closed; the UI says "This bid is locked." | N-M1-01, N-M1-02, NC-12 |
| New Condition NC-1 | `edit_condition_dialog.py:858`, `:1480-1508` | shows Cond. No. 0, refuses it, drops the typed number | read-only "Next number: N", RefNo = MAX of the bid + 1 | N-M1-07 |
| NC-2 | `condition_action_handler.py:547-551` | default layer = first "Default" of the merged list, shared rows included; a bid without its own "Default" refuses every create | first own layer named exactly "Default", else none | safe |
| NC-3 | `condition_action_handler.py:758`; `edit_condition_dialog.py:1829-1839` | `_dirty` forced, Cancel of an untouched form asks to save | silent close when untouched; an edited form asks "Discard this new condition?", also on swipe-down | N-M1-09 |

| SS-11, SS-12 | `mdb_writer.py:63-105`; `project_write_service.py:373-379` | a batch is all-or-nothing only by a side channel; the bid is derived from the page | one transaction; a page of another bid refused | integrity |
| Set Scale UI | `set_scale_dialog.py:51-132`; `ui_event_coordinator.py:5401-5409`, `:5496-5510`, `:5597-5643` | inline combo, OK, Cancel and Apply; the active-page context; no confirmation | pull-down `UIMenu`, Cancel and Save; the pressed page; one confirmation for "apply to all" over several pages | N-M1-21 |
| Undo replay | `plan_view_action_handler.py:1667-1673` | an unknown page answers the pair (1.0, 1.0), which turns into the current ratio | nothing captured replays with factor 1; a page that is gone at replay refuses the undo or redo | N-M1-06 |

Kept as Windows on purpose (each pinned by a test): RP-1 (the stripped typed text is compared with the unstripped stored name) and RP-4 (no live re-sort); DP-7, DP-11 and the asymmetry of steps 13 and 14 of the delete cascade (the selected-page and cover-sheet clears are not bid scoped, steps 3 to 6 are, step 9 is not); LY-12 (a move on equal Sequence changes nothing); NC-11 (no uniqueness rule on create); CF-9 (the list keeps the sidebar order, M3 sorts the CSV by the summary rule); CF-11 (the in-use wording names the selected folder); SS-6 to SS-9 and SS-13 (re-quantised takeoffs, only `ScaleFactor1` and `ScaleFactor2` written, a non-numeric Position row skipped, the 1e-9 epsilon exclusive); the Windows quirk COND-STYLE-02 (a new Area gets the Spacing field value).

### 9a.6 Windows defects and oddities not copied

The rows of section 8 whose "safe behaviour kept" was built are the rows of the table above; the register stays the list to hand to `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json`. Not built on purpose: NC-7 (a new type is committed before validation) because M1 has no type creation (N-M1-11). New findings of the build that are not rows of section 8, with the ids the builders used:

- **NC-8b**: `condition_operations.py:326-334` reads the type uid with `int()`, which accepts " 7" and "+7" (the layer and folder uids are digits only in Windows too, `bid_owned_identity.py:112`); iOS accepts ASCII digits above zero.
- **CF-1b**: `project_write_service.py:876-893` skips the blocked folders and deletes the rest, and `_condition_folder_uids_in_use` treats a requested uid that is no folder (a dangling `ParentUID`, the text "0") in its own way; iOS refuses the whole batch and reproduces the Windows walk exactly (13 oracle rows from the real function, `ConditionFolderOracleData.hpp`).
- **LY-4b**: `project_write_service.py:6884-6892` drops empty uids and keeps "7" and "007" as two uids in a layer batch delete; iOS refuses the whole delete.
- **RP-1b**: the Windows writer (`page_operations.py:130-152`) accepts any string; only the dialog strips and refuses blank. iOS re-checks in the store, and refuses a page uid that exists in two bids.
- **SS-10 addendum**: the four differences between the in-place legend scanner and expat (see the table); a fuzz test fails on any new kind of disagreement.
- **DP asymmetry**: steps 13 and 14 of the delete cascade are not bid scoped in Windows (`settings_operations.py:866-889`) while steps 3 to 6 are; kept and pinned, now documented.
- **NUL in text**: a NUL in a condition name or notes, which the iOS loader would cut (C strings); refused.
- **Windows `condition_operations.py:331`** writes Notes as a BLOB (UTF-8); iOS writes TEXT (4.5), which M3 must round-trip.
- **iOS-side finding, not a Windows oddity**: `OstDatabase::open` uses the default open flags, so a refused call on a path that does not exist creates an empty database file. Not reachable in the app (it always passes `ost_projects.sqlite`); suggestion: open writers read-write without create.

### 9a.7 Open points that need the owner's decision

1. **New Layer loses the typed text when the name is refused.** The alert is closed when the rule runs, so after a duplicate, "Comments", a 51-unit or a control-character name only an OK alert remains and the name must be typed again. Windows keeps the pending new row in its editor (`layers_dialog.py:433-436`, `layers_sidebar.py:349-351`); the Rename Page alert of the same milestone returns with the draft. Choose: keep (cheapest) or make the New Layer alert return with the draft like Rename Page.
2. **The page menu against the whole-view long-press recogniser (device check M1-14).** The guard in `_handle3DLongPress:` only stops the action; it cannot stop the recogniser competing with the context-menu interaction of a page button in 3D mode. If M1-14 shows the menu does not open: in `ViewController.mm`, where the recogniser is made (`:255-260`), set `longPress.delegate = self`, adopt `UIGestureRecognizerDelegate` in the class extension, and implement `gestureRecognizer:shouldReceiveTouch:` returning NO for a touch inside `pageBar3D` or `pageBar2D` (the camera recognisers have a method of the same name in `CameraController`: do not mix them); keep the guard.
3. **Rise, Run and Display Size % use the decimal pad** (no minus key, only the locale's separator): a negative Linear rise or run cannot be typed, and in a comma locale '.' cannot be typed while Core refuses ','. Pre-existing in the Edit dialog; New Condition opens as Linear, so it inherits it. Set Scale avoided the same trap (N-M1-22). Choose: leave, or use the numbers-and-punctuation keyboard for those fields.
4. **"New Condition in <folder>" for a folder that holds only sub-folders**: such a folder has no section in the list, so the item is unreachable for it (use a sub-folder, the top level or Move to Folder). Choose: leave, or add the item to the folder manager.
5. **Set Scale saves synchronously on the main thread**, and the undo capture is O(n x m) over the project takeoffs (a group move of 2000 takeoffs in a 50,000-takeoff project is a few tenths of a second). Windows saves asynchronously. Judge with the largest bid in M1-17; a background queue with a disabled form is the follow-up.
6. **A page with a NULL or 0 stored scale** opens the form as 1 : 1 (the loader coalesces 0 to 1.0) and every Save on it is refused with the generic "Save Failed" text (N-M1-22, SS-1); with "apply to all pages" one such page fails the whole save and nothing names it (the store's own text only goes to the log). No page of the pack has such a scale. Choose: leave, name the page in the alert, or a repair tool (needs your word).
7. **`JobStatuses` text that is not valid UTF-8** (`ImportService_Internal.h` notes that older databases can hold windows-1252 text) empties the status list, so every bid with a status reads as locked and the UI says "This bid is locked." Fail closed as planned, but the message blames the bid. Choose: leave, give the failed read its own text, or read such text tolerantly.
8. **After a committed write whose re-read failed**, the in-memory lists stay old and every writer stays enabled (the alerts tell the user to reopen the bid); a second New Condition after a failed refresh adds the second row to the list while the first stays missing until reopen, and the Layers sheet keeps stale sequences. Rare; choose: leave, or close the sheet after such an alert.
9. **Set Scale memory twin against a reload**: a takeoff dragged or placed in the same session holds the exact double in memory while the database holds the 3-decimal text, so after a scale change memory differs from a reload by up to 0.0005 x factor in those coordinates until the bid is reopened (the same drift already exists after any drag); and the safety net of the twin (the twin refuses after the database was written) answers "saved" if the re-read fails. Both are not reachable by design.
10. **The 2D / 3D chips** are dimmed by `_highlightSelection` on every page-button tap (pre-existing); M1 repaints them only after a rename, a delete and a Set Scale mismatch, so the wording "the chips keep the active-mode colour" of M1-14 and M1-15 holds right after those actions and not after the next page tap.
11. **The rename rule order**: blank, then NUL or invalid UTF-8, then too long, then unchanged, so a stored name over 255 units typed again unchanged answers "too long" (pinned, flagged in the test).
12. **Pack and check gaps**: no pack page has a NULL or 0 stored scale (point 6), and no check opens the page menu while the Conditions or Layers sheet is up at its medium detent, where the page bars stay tappable. N-M1-26 (an iPad for M1-01, M1-13, M1-19) is still your answer.
13. **The lock guard covers only the new M1 writes** (N-M1-01): takeoff placement and edit, and condition edit, delete and duplicate on a locked bid stay allowed on iOS. Choose when to retrofit.
14. **Housekeeping**: the untracked empty `a.out` in the repository root is not ignored by `.gitignore`: delete it and commit with explicit paths; `+renamePageWithUID:inBid:toName:` (without the error out-parameter) and `_presentSetScaleForCurrentPage` are declared and never called; the unused-API cleanup is yours to call.
15. **CI first (answered by run 45528720676, Updated 2026-11-24 (job 8, M1))**: the first-party warning count of the CI run was the authority for the selector scan of 9a.3 item 3 (the baseline of 9 distinct; the scan showed one `-Wselector` for `_layersAddTapped:` on top of it); the real-SDK compile of run 45528720676 printed the 9 distinct first-party warnings of the baseline and none for `_layersAddTapped:`, with no error. The known case `+[UIAction actionWithHandler:]` (five uses in M1, none before; a residual of the recheck of the fixes) compiled: CI reported no unknown class method, so the fallback to `+[UIAction actionWithTitle:image:identifier:handler:]`, the form already used in `ConditionFoldersViewController.mm`, is not needed. What a compile cannot show, behaviour at run time, stays for the device checks.

**What and why.** Branch `ios-parity-sync-2` again (start `fc3c3b6` = the documents tip of pass 3, code tip of pass 3 `b36f2f6`; Windows `d1c430a`, no new commit since pass 3). **The three decisions (all recorded with the date in `PORTING_BLOCKERS.md`, `PARITY_MAP.md` and `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json`; the questions are closed).**
1. **EXP-21, export and import alert wording: adopt the current Windows wording** (section 4.15 holds the rule and the sentences). Every alert shows ONE user sentence; the technical reason goes to the log only. Applied to the import alerts (title "Import Error", the single local sentence "The OST file is damaged or uses an unsupported format, so it cannot be imported." for EVERY local failure (store, schema and temporary-folder failures too, as Windows), OSP likewise, the unexpected sentence only for a C++ exception or a nil error, the open-in line "<file name>: ..."), the picker and load failures ("Error Loading File" with a user reason), the folder delete failure ("Delete Error" / "Failed to delete projects. The database may be locked by another application."; "Cannot Delete Project" only for a folder that still has bids), the page-delete reload alert (iOS sentence kept, reason dropped) and the export alerts ("Export Error" with the three sentences; "Nothing to Export" and "Error creating <fmt> export:" are gone). Not wired, for M3: the known-cause sentences (not found, access denied, disk full), because the writers return bools and the store returns text only; the Core vocabulary (`FileFailureCause`, `cause_from_errno`, `write_failure_text`) exists with host tests. The sentence with "of the <fmt> file" is added with M3 (4.15).
2. **Layer failure and refresh alerts: take the Windows dialog titles and texts** (`layers_dialog.py:489`, `:784`, `:677-694`, `:706-722`): the refresh-failed title of a rename is now "Rename Layer" and of a move "Move Layer" (it was "Refresh Error"; the texts are unchanged); the generic failures "Failed to create|rename|move|delete layer." lose the DB-locked hint, which removes that part of N-M1-17 (the Windows dialog never has it). Unchanged: the create refresh failure ("Refresh Error"), the delete refresh failure ("Delete Layer"), "Duplicate Layer", "Failed to validate layer usage.", the three "Layer Visibility" alerts and the iOS-only refusals (50 characters, control characters, sequence limit, "This bid is locked.").
3. **"Count as Negative Quantity" on a pasted group (replaces the pressed-takeoff-only default of UND-09 for a group; a single takeoff is unchanged).** The Windows "group" is the current multi-selection (after Paste the pasted takeoffs, children included, stay selected). Rule (`plan_view_action_handler.py:2134-2181`, `input_handler.py:3247-3251`, `view_context_menu.py:162-196`): the item is offered only if the selection holds at least one root takeoff and NO child takeoff (a hole or an attachment); it is checked only if EVERY selected takeoff is negative; the new value is "not all negative" for EVERY selected takeoff (mixed or all positive: all become negative; all negative: all become positive), not the value of the pressed item; ONE undo entry records old and new for ALL members, including those already at the target. Ported in Core now: `negative_toggle_for_press(takeoffs, group_uids, pressed_uid)` in `Core/Domain/NegativeChange.hpp` (group path for 2 or more uids that include the pressed takeoff; the single path is exactly the old behaviour, no hole gate: iOS still offers the item on one pressed hole or attachment, Windows hides it, a recorded divergence). iOS has no multi-selection and no paste before M2: the long-press handler passes an EMPTY group (identical behaviour); the M2 paste wiring passes the pasted uids as the group (5.7, N-M2-04). Tests: suite `Und09_NegativeGroup`, 9 tests in `Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp` (all-positive group, mixed group with the pressed takeoff negative, all-negative group, hole or attachment member, non-member press, annotation or unknown uids skipped, duplicate uids once, a TempDb chain test: one entry, one undo, one redo); the six old single-takeoff tests stay valid. Owner flags (defaults = exact Windows): a pasted group containing a hole or attachment has no item; the check mark shows only when all are negative.

## 10. Out of scope

**The owner's list, verbatim:** SQL Server collaboration, AI takeoff, payroll / employees / job statuses / cover sheet / bid areas, the tower crane, detached windows, FBX.

**What stays out even if it looks adjacent** (nothing here is planned, started or estimated):

- **Duplicate Bid**, new bid, bid import of pages, bid comparison, project-tree editing beyond what exists; the bid areas UI and area editing (only the READ-ONLY `BidAreas` load of the Summary CSV, N-M3-02, if you allow it); the cover sheet and its page selection / overlay setter; job statuses (only the read of `JobStatuses.Locked` for the lock guard); payroll / employees (only the referenced `Employees`, `PayClasses` and `AccessLevels` rows that the OST / OSP export must carry for a faithful round trip, with the Employees scrub of N-M3-10; no payroll feature).
- **Plan Organizer** (add, duplicate, import, move pages and folders) and page folders beyond what rename and delete page need; page name display options.
- **Default (template) layers** maintenance (N-M1-18) and condition types (`CdnTypes`) master data, including creating a type by typing a new one (N-M1-11).
- **Condition numbering features**: Renumber Conditions, Duplicate and Reassign, multi-condition Duplicate, condition cut / copy / paste in the Conditions list (a same-bid cut is a multi-condition move onto a folder or type group; copy + paste is a duplicate into a target) and across bids, as a USER feature (cross-bid TAKEOFF paste in M2 copies conditions only as part of the paste, N-M2-05); `ost_store::shift_condition_ref_nos` stays unused.
- **Selection and editing extras**: marquee selection, Select All, rotate / flip takeoffs, slope handle, curved placement, attachment placement, zoom tool, PDF text selection, undo of anything beyond takeoffs and annotations (page writes have no undo, N-M1-06).
- **Rendering extras**: condition labels on the 2D plan (N-M4-23), TIFF raster pages as page backgrounds (TIFF is only an overlay file type, N-M4-16), the 3D page-image plane, HTML-export toggles (Edges, cut sliders), saved per-bid 3D camera state, separate 2D / 3D display modes.
- **Scale tools**: calibrate from a measured length (not a Windows feature; N-M1-22).
- **Annotation extras**: drawing `BidCallOuts` (kept and round-tripped only), detached annotation / view windows.
- **Platform**: Access `.mdb`, licensing, updates, installer, menus and shortcuts, MCP helpers, credential storage, `UIPasteboard`, the OS clipboard.

End of plan.
